forward_data: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> grad_input: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    size: u32,\n    alpha: f32,\n    beta: f32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\nconst PI: f32 = 3.14159265358979323846;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn relu_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n hardswish_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < params.size) {\n        let x = forward_data[idx];\n        var grad: f32 = 0.0;\n        if (x const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.AdaptiveAvgPool2DSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct AdaptivePoolParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    channels: u32,\n    in_height: u32,\n    in_width: u32,\n    out_height: u32,\n    out_width: u32,\n    _pad1: u32,\n    _pad2: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: AdaptivePoolParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)\nfn adaptive_avg_pool2d(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let out_x = gid.x;\n    let out_y = gid.y;\n    let c = gid.z % params.channels;\n    let batch = gid.z / params.channels;\n    if (out_x >= params.out_width || out_y >= params.out_height || batch >= params.batch_size) { return; }\n    // Compute bin boundaries using floor/ceil for proper adaptive pooling\n    let start_h = u32(floor(f32(out_y * params.in_height) / f32(params.out_height)));\n    let end_h = u32(ceil(f32((out_y + 1u) * params.in_height) / f32(params.out_height)));\n    let start_w = u32(floor(f32(out_x * params.in_width) / f32(params.out_width)));\n    let end_w = u32(ceil(f32((out_x + 1u) * params.in_width) / f32(params.out_width)));\n    var sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    var count: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var ih: u32 = start_h; ih < end_h; ih = ih + 1u) {\n        for (var iw: u32 = start_w; iw < end_w; iw = iw + 1u) {\n            let in_idx = batch * params.channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                       + c * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                       + ih * params.in_width + iw;\n            sum = sum + input[in_idx];\n            count = count + 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n    let out_idx = batch * params.channels * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                + c * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                + out_y * params.out_width + out_x;\n    output[out_idx] = sum / max(count, 1.0);\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.AdditionalOptimizerSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read_write> params_arr: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> gradients: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> state1: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> state2: array<f32>;\n\nstruct OptimizerParams {\n    size: u32,\n    lr: f32,\n    beta1: f32,\n    beta2: f32,\n    epsilon: f32,\n    weight_decay: f32,\n    t: u32,\n    extra: f32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> opt_params: OptimizerParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn rmsprop(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < opt_params.size) {\n        let grad = gradients[idx] + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx];\n        // state1 = squared_avg, beta1 = rho\n        state1[idx] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad * grad;\n        params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - opt_params.lr * grad / (sqrt(state1[idx]) + opt_params.epsilon);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn adagrad(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < opt_params.size) {\n        let grad = gradients[idx] + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx];\n        state1[idx] = state1[idx] + grad * grad;\n        params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - opt_params.lr * grad / (sqrt(state1[idx]) + opt_params.epsilon);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn nag(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < opt_params.size) {\n        let grad = gradients[idx] + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx];\n        let v_prev = state1[idx];\n        state1[idx] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[idx] - opt_params.lr * grad;\n        params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] + (-opt_params.beta1 * v_prev + (1.0 + opt_params.beta1) * state1[idx]);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn adadelta(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < opt_params.size) {\n        let grad = gradients[idx] + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx];\n        // state1 = accum_grad, state2 = accum_update, beta1 = rho\n        state1[idx] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad * grad;\n        let update = sqrt(state2[idx] + opt_params.epsilon) / sqrt(state1[idx] + opt_params.epsilon) * grad;\n        state2[idx] = opt_params.beta1 * state2[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * update * update;\n        params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - update;\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn adamax(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < opt_params.size) {\n        let grad = gradients[idx] + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx];\n        let bc1 = 1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, f32(opt_params.t));\n        state1[idx] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n        state2[idx] = max(opt_params.beta2 * state2[idx], abs(grad));\n        params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - opt_params.lr / bc1 * state1[idx] / (state2[idx] + opt_params.epsilon);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn lion(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < opt_params.size) {\n        let update = sign(opt_params.beta1 * state1[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * gradients[idx]);\n        params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - opt_params.lr * (update + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx]);\n        state1[idx] = opt_params.beta2 * state1[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * gradients[idx];\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn nadam(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < opt_params.size) {\n        let grad = gradients[idx] + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx];\n        let step = f32(opt_params.t);\n        let bc1 = 1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, step);\n        let bc1_next = 1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, step + 1.0);\n        let bc2 = 1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta2, step);\n        state1[idx] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n        state2[idx] = opt_params.beta2 * state2[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad * grad;\n        let v_hat = state2[idx] / bc2;\n        // Dozat 2016 Alg. 2 (constant mu): look-ahead momentum by bc1_next, current gradient by bc1.\n        params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - opt_params.lr * (opt_params.beta1 * state1[idx] / bc1_next + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad / bc1) / (sqrt(v_hat) + opt_params.epsilon);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.AmsgradOptimizerSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read_write> params_arr: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> gradients: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> m: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> v: array<f32>;\n\nstruct OptimizerParams {\n    size: u32,\n    lr: f32,\n    beta1: f32,\n    beta2: f32,\n    epsilon: f32,\n    weight_decay: f32,\n    t: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> opt_params: OptimizerParams;\n\n// Note: v_max is stored as the second half of the v buffer (offset by size)\n// This avoids needing a 5th storage buffer binding\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn amsgrad(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < opt_params.size) {\n        let grad = gradients[idx] + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx];\n        let step = f32(opt_params.t);\n        let bc1 = 1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, step);\n        let bc2 = 1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta2, step);\n        m[idx] = opt_params.beta1 * m[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n        v[idx] = opt_params.beta2 * v[idx] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad * grad;\n        // v_max stored at offset size\n        let vm_idx = idx + opt_params.size;\n        v[vm_idx] = max(v[vm_idx], v[idx]);\n        params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - opt_params.lr * (m[idx] / bc1) / (sqrt(v[vm_idx] / bc2) + opt_params.epsilon);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.AttentionBackwardResidentSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> backward_grad_out: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> backward_query: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> backward_key: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> backward_value: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read> backward_weights: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read> backward_bias: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(6) var<storage, read_write> backward_grad_primary: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(7) var<storage, read_write> backward_grad_secondary: array<f32>;\n\nstruct AttentionBackwardResidentParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    num_q_heads: u32,\n    num_kv_heads: u32,\n    seq_q: u32,\n    seq_k: u32,\n    head_dim: u32,\n    queries_per_kv: u32,\n    is_causal: u32,\n    scale: f32,\n    has_bias: u32,\n    bias_batch_stride: u32,\n    use_weights: u32,\n    _pad0: u32,\n    _pad1: u32,\n    _pad2: u32,\n    _pad3: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(8) var<uniform> backward_params: AttentionBackwardResidentParams;\n\nfn attention_backward_resident_score(b: u32, q_head: u32, kv_head: u32, q_pos: u32, k_pos: u32) -> f32 {\n    if (backward_params.is_causal != 0u && k_pos > q_pos) {\n        return -3.402823e+38;\n    }\n\n    let q_base = ((b * backward_params.num_q_heads + q_head) * backward_params.seq_q + q_pos)\n        * backward_params.head_dim;\n    let k_base = ((b * backward_params.num_kv_heads + kv_head) * backward_params.seq_k + k_pos)\n        * backward_params.head_dim;\n    var dot: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var d: u32 = 0u; d < backward_params.head_dim; d = d + 1u) {\n        dot = dot + backward_query[q_base + d] * backward_key[k_base + d];\n    }\n\n    var score = dot * backward_params.scale;\n    if (backward_params.has_bias != 0u) {\n        var bias_base: u32 = 0u;\n        if (backward_params.bias_batch_stride != 0u) {\n            bias_base = b * backward_params.bias_batch_stride;\n        }\n        let bias_idx = bias_base\n            + q_head * backward_params.seq_q * backward_params.seq_k\n            + q_pos * backward_params.seq_k + k_pos;\n        score = score + backward_bias[bias_idx];\n    }\n    return score;\n}\n\nfn attention_backward_resident_weight(\n    row: u32, b: u32, q_head: u32, kv_head: u32, q_pos: u32, k_pos: u32,\n    max_score: f32, sum_exp: f32) -> f32 {\n    if (backward_params.use_weights != 0u) {\n        return backward_weights[row * backward_params.seq_k + k_pos];\n    }\n    return exp(attention_backward_resident_score(b, q_head, kv_head, q_pos, k_pos) - max_score) / sum_exp;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn attention_backward_resident_query(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = backward_params.batch_size * backward_params.num_q_heads\n        * backward_params.seq_q * backward_params.head_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n\n    let d = idx % backward_params.head_dim;\n    let row = idx / backward_params.head_dim;\n    let q_pos = row % backward_params.seq_q;\n    let q_head = (row / backward_params.seq_q) % backward_params.num_q_heads;\n    let b = row / (backward_params.seq_q * backward_params.num_q_heads);\n    let kv_head = q_head / backward_params.queries_per_kv;\n    let grad_out_base = row * backward_params.head_dim;\n    let value_head_base = (b * backward_params.num_kv_heads + kv_head)\n        * backward_params.seq_k * backward_params.head_dim;\n\n    var max_score: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    var sum_exp: f32 = 0.0;\n    if (backward_params.use_weights == 0u) {\n        for (var k_pos: u32 = 0u; k_pos < backward_params.seq_k; k_pos = k_pos + 1u) {\n            max_score = max(max_score,\n                attention_backward_resident_score(b, q_head, kv_head, q_pos, k_pos));\n        }\n        for (var k_pos: u32 = 0u; k_pos < backward_params.seq_k; k_pos = k_pos + 1u) {\n            sum_exp = sum_exp + exp(\n                attention_backward_resident_score(b, q_head, kv_head, q_pos, k_pos) - max_score);\n        }\n    }\n\n    var weighted_grad_sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var k_pos: u32 = 0u; const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.AttentionBackwardSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> grad_out: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> query: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> key: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> value: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read_write> grad_query: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read_write> grad_kv: array<f32>;\n\nstruct AttnBwdParams {\n    batch_heads: u32,\n    seq_len: u32,\n    head_dim: u32,\n    _pad: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(6) var<uniform> params: AttnBwdParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn attention_backward_query(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_heads * params.seq_len * params.head_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let d = idx % params.head_dim;\n    let q_pos = (idx / params.head_dim) % params.seq_len;\n    let bh = idx / (params.head_dim * params.seq_len);\n    let scale = 1.0 / sqrt(f32(params.head_dim));\n    let qkv_base = bh * params.seq_len * params.head_dim;\n    let q_base = qkv_base + q_pos * params.head_dim;\n    // Recompute attention weights for this query position\n    var max_score: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    for (var k_pos: u32 = 0u; k_pos < params.seq_len; k_pos = k_pos + 1u) {\n        var dot: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.head_dim; i = i + 1u) {\n            dot += query[q_base + i] * key[qkv_base + k_pos * params.head_dim + i];\n        }\n        max_score = max(max_score, dot * scale);\n    }\n    var sum_exp: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var k_pos: u32 = 0u; k_pos < params.seq_len; k_pos = k_pos + 1u) {\n        var dot: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.head_dim; i = i + 1u) {\n            dot += query[q_base + i] * key[qkv_base + k_pos * params.head_dim + i];\n        }\n        sum_exp += exp(dot * scale - max_score);\n    }\n    // Compute dQ[q_pos, d] = sum_k attn_weight[q_pos, k_pos] * (scale * K[k_pos, d] * dS)\n    // where dS[q_pos, k_pos] = sum_d' (grad_out[q_pos, d'] * V[k_pos, d'])\n    // Then dQ = sum_k softmax_grad * K[k_pos, d] * scale\n    var grad_q_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var k_pos: u32 = 0u; dd < params.head_dim; dd = dd + 1u) {\n            d_score += grad_out[qkv_base + q_pos * params.head_dim + dd] * value[qkv_base + k_pos * params.head_dim + dd];\n        }\n        // Softmax backward: dAttn = attn_w * (dScore - sum_j(attn_j * dScore_j))\n        // We approximate by accumulating directly: dQ += dAttn * K * scale\n        // Full computation needs dAttn adjustment, but we compute dQ as:\n        // dQ[d] = scale * sum_k( attn_w * d_score * K[k, d] )\n        // Then subtract the weighted mean: - scale * sum_k( attn_w * weighted_sum * K[k, d] )\n        grad_q_val += attn_w * d_score * key[qkv_base + k_pos * params.head_dim + d] * scale;\n    }\n    // Correct with softmax Jacobian: subtract attn-weighted sum\n    var weighted_dscore_sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var k_pos: u32 = 0u; k_pos < params.seq_len; k_pos = k_pos + 1u) {\n        var dot: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.head_dim; i = i + 1u) {\n            dot += query[q_base + i] * key[qkv_base + k_pos * params.head_dim + i];\n        }\n        let attn_w = exp(dot * scale - max_score) / sum_exp;\n        var d_score: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var dd: u32 = 0u; k_pos < params.seq_len; k_pos = k_pos + 1u) {\n        var dot: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.head_dim; i = i + 1u) {\n            dot += query[q_base + i] * key[qkv_base + k_pos * params.head_dim + i];\n        }\n        let attn_w = exp(dot * scale - max_score) / sum_exp;\n        correction += attn_w * key[qkv_base + k_pos * params.head_dim + d] * scale;\n    }\n    grad_query[idx] = grad_q_val - weighted_dscore_sum * correction;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn attention_backward_kv(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_heads * params.seq_len * params.head_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let d = idx % params.head_dim;\n    let k_pos = (idx / params.head_dim) % params.seq_len;\n    let bh = idx / (params.head_dim * params.seq_len);\n    let scale = 1.0 / sqrt(f32(params.head_dim));\n    let qkv_base = bh * params.seq_len * params.head_dim;\n    // grad_kv: first half = gradKey, second half = gradValue\n    // Compute gradK[k_pos, d] and gradV[k_pos, d]\n    var grad_k_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    var grad_v_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var q_pos: u32 = 0u; q_pos < params.seq_len; q_pos = q_pos + 1u) {\n        let q_base = qkv_base + q_pos * params.head_dim;\n        // Recompute attention weight for (q_pos, k_pos)\n        var max_score: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n        for (var kk: u32 = 0u; kk < params.seq_len; kk = kk + 1u) {\n            var dot: f32 = 0.0;\n            for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.head_dim; i = i + 1u) {\n                dot += query[q_base + i] * key[qkv_base + kk * params.head_dim + i];\n            }\n            max_score = max(max_score, dot * scale);\n        }\n        var sum_exp: f32 = 0.0;\n        var this_exp: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var kk: u32 = 0u; kk < params.seq_len; kk = kk + 1u) {\n            var dot: f32 = 0.0;\n            for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.head_dim; i = i + 1u) {\n                dot += query[q_base + i] * key[qkv_base + kk * params.head_dim + i];\n            }\n            let e = exp(dot * scale - max_score);\n            sum_exp += e;\n            if (kk == k_pos) { this_exp = e; }\n        }\n        let attn_w = this_exp / sum_exp;\n        // dScore for softmax backward\n        var d_score: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var dd: u32 = 0u; dd < params.head_dim; dd = dd + 1u) {\n            d_score += grad_out[q_base + dd] * value[qkv_base + k_pos * params.head_dim + dd];\n        }\n        var weighted_dscore_sum: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var kk: u32 = 0u; kk < params.seq_len; kk = kk + 1u) {\n            var dot2: f32 = 0.0;\n            for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.head_dim; i = i + 1u) {\n                dot2 += query[q_base + i] * key[qkv_base + kk * params.head_dim + i];\n            }\n            let aw = exp(dot2 * scale - max_score) / sum_exp;\n            var ds: f32 = 0.0;\n            for (var dd: u32 = 0u; dd < params.head_dim; dd = dd + 1u) {\n                ds += grad_out[q_base + dd] * value[qkv_base + kk * params.head_dim + dd];\n            }\n            weighted_dscore_sum += aw * ds;\n        }\n        let d_attn = attn_w * (d_score - weighted_dscore_sum);\n        // gradK[k_pos, d] += d_attn * Q[q_pos, d] * scale\n        grad_k_val += d_attn * query[q_base + d] * scale;\n        // gradV[k_pos, d] += attn_w * grad_out[q_pos, d]\n        grad_v_val += attn_w * grad_out[q_base + d];\n    }\n    grad_kv[idx] = grad_k_val;\n    grad_kv[total + idx] = grad_v_val;\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.AttentionSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> query: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> key: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> value: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read> attention_bias: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read_write> attention_weights: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(6) var<storage, read_write> softmax_stats: array<f32>;\n\nstruct AttentionForwardParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    num_q_heads: u32,\n    num_kv_heads: u32,\n    seq_q: u32,\n    seq_k: u32,\n    head_dim: u32,\n    queries_per_kv: u32,\n    is_causal: u32,\n    scale: f32,\n    has_bias: u32,\n    bias_batch_stride: u32,\n    store_weights: u32,\n    store_stats: u32,\n    boolean_mask_mode: u32,\n    softcap: f32,\n    _pad3: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(7) var<uniform> attention_params: AttentionForwardParams;\n\nfn attention_forward_excluded(b: u32, q_head: u32, q_pos: u32, k_pos: u32) -> bool {\n    // KV-cache causal offset: query q_pos sits at absolute position q_pos + (seq_k - seq_q); seq_q==seq_k\n    // collapses to k_pos > q_pos (prefill), while a decode step attends to the whole cached prefix.\n    if (attention_params.is_causal != 0u && k_pos > q_pos + (attention_params.seq_k - attention_params.seq_q)) { return true; }\n\n    let q_base = ((b * attention_params.num_q_heads + q_head) * attention_params.seq_q + q_pos)\n        * attention_params.head_dim;\n    let k_base = ((b * attention_params.num_kv_heads + kv_head) * attention_params.seq_k + k_pos)\n        * attention_params.head_dim;\n    var dot: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var d: u32 = 0u; d < attention_params.head_dim; d = d + 1u) {\n        var result: f32 = 0.0;\n        for (var k_pos: u32 = 0u; k_pos < attention_params.seq_k; k_pos = k_pos + 1u) {\n            if (!attention_forward_excluded(b, q_head, q_pos, k_pos) && sum_exp > 0.0) {\n                let weight = exp(attention_forward_score(b, q_head, kv_head, q_pos, k_pos) - max_score) / sum_exp;\n                result = result + weight * value[value_head_base + k_pos * attention_params.head_dim + d];\n            }\n        }\n        output[out_base + d] = result;\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.BatchedGemmSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> A: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> B: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> C: array<f32>;\n\nstruct GemmParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    M: u32,\n    N: u32,\n    K: u32,\n    alpha: f32,\n    beta: f32,\n    _pad1: f32,\n    _pad2: f32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: GemmParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn batched_gemm(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let mat_size = params.M * params.N;\n    let total = params.batch_size * mat_size;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let b = idx / mat_size;\n    let local_idx = idx % mat_size;\n    let row = local_idx / params.N;\n    let col = local_idx % params.N;\n    let a_off = b * params.M * params.K;\n    let b_off = b * params.K * params.N;\n    let c_off = b * mat_size;\n    var acc: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var k: u32 = 0u; k < params.K; k = k + 1u) {\n        acc = acc + A[a_off + row * params.K + k] * B[b_off + k * params.N + col];\n    }\n    // beta = 0 must not read C (BLAS semantics): 0 * NaN or 0 * Inf from an uninitialized or reused buffer is NaN.\n    C[c_off + local_idx] = select(params.alpha * acc, params.alpha * acc + params.beta * C[c_off + local_idx], params.beta != 0.0);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn gemm_bias_activation(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.M * params.N;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let row = idx / params.N;\n    let col = idx % params.N;\n    var acc: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var k: u32 = 0u; k < params.K; k = k + 1u) {\n        acc = acc + A[row * params.K + k] * B[k * params.N + col];\n    }\n    // bias in C buffer (read at col), output written to C\n    let biased = acc + C[col];\n    // ReLU activation (alpha field used as activation flag: 0=none, 1=relu)\n    C[idx] = select(biased, max(0.0, biased), params.alpha > 0.5);\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.BatchNormStatsSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> out_mean: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> out_var: array<f32>;\n\nstruct BNStatsParams {\n    batch: u32,\n    channels: u32,\n    spatial: u32,\n    _pad: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: BNStatsParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn batch_norm_stats(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let c = gid.x;\n    if (c >= params.channels) { return; }\n    let count = params.batch * params.spatial;\n    var sum_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var n: u32 = 0u; n < params.batch; n = n + 1u) {\n        for (var s: u32 = 0u; s < params.spatial; s = s + 1u) {\n            sum_val = sum_val + input[(n * params.channels + c) * params.spatial + s];\n        }\n    }\n    let mean_val = sum_val / f32(count);\n    out_mean[c] = mean_val;\n    var var_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var n: u32 = 0u; n < params.batch; n = n + 1u) {\n        for (var s: u32 = 0u; s < params.spatial; s = s + 1u) {\n            let d = input[(n * params.channels + c) * params.spatial + s] - mean_val;\n            var_val = var_val + d * d;\n        }\n    }\n    out_var[c] = var_val / f32(count);\n}\n" -> string!

let dI1=hi*select(0.0,-1.0,py1>ty1)*iw;\n    let dI2=hi*select(0.0,1.0,px2<tx2)*ih; let dI3=hi*select(0.0,1.0,py2<ty2)*iw;\n    let dU0=-ph-dI0; let dU1=-pw-dI1; let dU2=ph-dI2; let dU3=pw-dI3;\n    let uSq=uA*uA;\n    let ig0=-(dI0*uA-iA*dU0)/uSq; let ig1=-(dI1*uA-iA*dU1)/uSq;\n    let ig2=-(dI2*uA-iA*dU2)/uSq; let ig3=-(dI3*uA-iA*dU3)/uSq;\n    let dx=0.5*(px1+px2)-0.5*(tx1+tx2); let dy=0.5*(py1+py2)-0.5*(ty1+ty2);\n    let rhoSq=dx*dx+dy*dy;\n    let encDx=max(px2,tx2)-min(px1,tx1); let encDy=max(py2,ty2)-min(py1,ty1);\n    let cSq=encDx*encDx+encDy*encDy+1e-7; let cSqSq=cSq*cSq;\n    let dCSq0=2.0*encDx*select(0.0,-1.0,px1<tx1); let dCSq1=2.0*encDy*select(0.0,-1.0,py1<ty1);\n    let dCSq2=2.0*encDx*select(0.0,1.0,px2>tx2); let dCSq3=2.0*encDy*select(0.0,1.0,py2>ty2);\n    let tw=max(tx2-tx1,1e-7); let th=max(ty2-ty1,1e-7);\n    let atanDiff=atan(tw/th)-atan(pw/ph); let fourOverPiSq=4.0/(3.14159265*3.14159265);\n    let v=fourOverPiSq*atanDiff*atanDiff;\n    let iou=iA/uA;\n    let alpha=v/(1.0-iou+v+1e-7);\n    let rss=pw*pw+ph*ph; let dAtanPw=ph/rss; let dAtanPh=-pw/rss;\n    let dV0=2.0*fourOverPiSq*atanDiff*dAtanPw; let dV1=2.0*fourOverPiSq*atanDiff*dAtanPh;\n    let dV2=2.0*fourOverPiSq*atanDiff*(-dAtanPw); let dV3=2.0*fourOverPiSq*atanDiff*(-dAtanPh);\n    let dp0=(dx*cSq-rhoSq*dCSq0)/cSqSq; NaN is tested on the bits (magnitude above\n        // the +Inf pattern): WGSL implementations may assume finite values and fold x != x.\n        let F32_ABS_MASK = 0x7fffffffu;       // IEEE-754 binary32: every bit but the sign\n        let F32_POS_INF_BITS = 0x7f800000u;   // IEEE-754 binary32: +Inf\n        B[idx] = select(clamp(x, params.min_val, params.max_val), x, (bitcast<u32>(x) & F32_ABS_MASK) > F32_POS_INF_BITS);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.CommonSource = "\n// WGSL Common Utilities\nconst PI: f32 = 3.14159265358979323846;\nconst E: f32 = 2.71828182845904523536;\nconst EPSILON: f32 = 1e-7;\nconst NEG_INF: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\nconst POS_INF: f32 = 3.402823e+38;\n\n// Fast approximation of tanh\nfn fast_tanh(x: f32) -> f32 {\n    let x2 = x * x;\n    let a = x * (135135.0 + x2 * (17325.0 + x2 * (378.0 + x2)));\n    let b = 135135.0 + x2 * (62370.0 + x2 * (3150.0 + x2 * 28.0));\n    return a / b;\n}\n\n// Fast approximation of sigmoid\nfn fast_sigmoid(x: f32) -> f32 {\n    return 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-x));\n}\n\n// GELU activation\nfn gelu(x: f32) -> f32 {\n    return 0.5 * x * (1.0 + tanh(sqrt(2.0 / PI) * (x + 0.044715 * x * x * x)));\n}\n\n// SiLU/Swish activation\nfn silu(x: f32) -> f32 {\n    return x / (1.0 + exp(-x));\n}\n" -> string!

array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> C: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    size: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn greater_than(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < params.size) {\n        C[idx] = select(0.0, 1.0, A[idx] > B[idx]);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn less_than(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < params.size) {\n        C[idx] = select(0.0, 1.0, A[idx] < B[idx]);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn equal_to(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < params.size) {\n        let ab = bitcast<u32>(A[idx]);\n        let bb = bitcast<u32>(B[idx]);\n        let aa = ab & 0x7fffffffu;\n        let ba = bb & 0x7fffffffu;\n        let equal = aa <= 0x7f800000u && ba <= 0x7f800000u\n            && (ab == bb || ((aa | ba) == 0u));\n        C[idx] = select(0.0, 1.0, equal);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn not_equal_to(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n        let equal = aa <= 0x7f800000u && ba <= 0x7f800000u\n            && (ab == bb || ((aa | ba) == 0u));\n        C[idx] = select(1.0, 0.0, equal);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn where_op(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < params.size) {\n        // A is condition, B is true_val, C is false_val (overwritten with result)\n        let condition = A[idx];\n        let true_val = B[idx];\n        let false_val = C[idx];\n        C[idx] = select(false_val, true_val, condition != 0.0);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

No.\n    // Actually for quantum rotation, we pass angles in a separate way.\n    // Use a simpler approach: A=real, B=imag, angles are cos/sin pre-computed in C buffer\n    // This kernel handles element-wise rotation: out = (cos*re - sin*im, sin*re + cos*im)\n    let c_val = cos(A[idx + total]);\n    let s_val = sin(A[idx + total]);\n    C[idx] = c_val * A[idx] - s_val * B[idx];\n    D[idx] = s_val * A[idx] + c_val * B[idx];\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn apply_window(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < params.size) { C[idx] = A[idx] * B[idx]; }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.CompressedOptimizerBf16Source = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read_write> params_arr: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> gradients: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> m_bits: array<atomic<u32>>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> v_bits: array<atomic<u32>>;\n\nstruct OptimizerParams {\n    size: u32,\n    lr: f32,\n    beta1: f32,\n    beta2: f32,\n    epsilon: f32,\n    weight_decay: f32,\n    t: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> opt_params: OptimizerParams;\n\nfn bf16_to_float(x: u32) -> f32 {\n    return bitcast<f32>(x << 16u);\n}\n\nfn float_to_bf16_rne(x: f32) -> u32 {\n    let bits = bitcast<u32>(x);\n    if ((bits & 0x7fffffffu) > 0x7f800000u) {\n        return ((bits >> 16u) | 0x40u) & 0xffffu;\n    }\n    let rounding = 0x7fffu + ((bits >> 16u) & 1u);\n    return ((bits + rounding) >> 16u) & 0xffffu;\n}\n\nfn load_m(idx: u32) -> u32 {\n    let word = atomicLoad(&m_bits[idx >> 1u]);\n    let shift = (idx & 1u) * 16u;\n    return (word >> shift) & 0xffffu;\n}\n\nfn load_v(idx: u32) -> u32 {\n    let word = atomicLoad(&v_bits[idx >> 1u]);\n    let shift = (idx & 1u) * 16u;\n    return (word >> shift) & 0xffffu;\n}\n\nfn store_m(idx: u32, value: u32) {\n    let word_idx = idx >> 1u;\n    let shift = (idx & 1u) * 16u;\n    let mask = 0xffffu << shift;\n    loop {\n        let old_word = atomicLoad(&m_bits[word_idx]);\n        let new_word = (old_word & ~mask) | ((value & 0xffffu) << shift);\n        let result = atomicCompareExchangeWeak(&m_bits[word_idx], old_word, new_word);\n        if (result.exchanged) {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn store_v(idx: u32, value: u32) {\n    let word_idx = idx >> 1u;\n    let shift = (idx & 1u) * 16u;\n    let mask = 0xffffu << shift;\n    loop {\n        let old_word = atomicLoad(&v_bits[word_idx]);\n        let new_word = (old_word & ~mask) | ((value & 0xffffu) << shift);\n        let result = atomicCompareExchangeWeak(&v_bits[word_idx], old_word, new_word);\n        if (result.exchanged) {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn adam_bf16(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= opt_params.size) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    var grad = gradients[idx];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) {\n        grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[idx];\n    }\n    let first_step = opt_params.t <= 1u;\n    let old_m = select(bf16_to_float(load_m(idx)), 0.0, first_step);\n    let old_v = select(bf16_to_float(load_v(idx)), 0.0, first_step);\n    let m_new = opt_params.beta1 * old_m + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n    let v_new = opt_params.beta2 * old_v + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad * grad;\n    store_m(idx, float_to_bf16_rne(m_new));\n    store_v(idx, float_to_bf16_rne(v_new));\n\n    let m_hat = m_new / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, f32(opt_params.t)));\n    let v_hat = v_new / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta2, f32(opt_params.t)));\n    params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - opt_params.lr * m_hat / (sqrt(v_hat) + opt_params.epsilon);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn adamw_bf16(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= opt_params.size) {\n opt_params.beta1 * old_m + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n    let v_new = opt_params.beta2 * old_v + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad * grad;\n    store_m(idx, float_to_bf16_rne(m_new));\n    store_v(idx, float_to_bf16_rne(v_new));\n\n    let m_hat = m_new / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, f32(opt_params.t)));\n    let v_hat = v_new / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta2, f32(opt_params.t)));\n    params_arr[idx] = params_arr[idx] - opt_params.lr * m_hat / (sqrt(v_hat) + opt_params.epsilon);\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.ConvTranspose2DBackwardInputSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> grad_output: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> kernel: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> grad_input: array<f32>;\n\nstruct ConvParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    in_channels: u32,\n    out_channels: u32,\n    in_height: u32,\n    in_width: u32,\n    out_height: u32,\n    out_width: u32,\n    kernel_height: u32,\n    kernel_width: u32,\n    stride_h: u32,\n    stride_w: u32,\n    pad_h: u32,\n    pad_w: u32,\n    dilation_h: u32,\n    dilation_w: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: ConvParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)\nfn conv_transpose2d_backward_input(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let in_x = gid.x;\n    let in_y = gid.y;\n    let ic = gid.z % params.in_channels;\n    let batch = gid.z / params.in_channels;\n\n    if (in_x >= params.in_width || in_y >= params.in_height || batch >= params.batch_size) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // ConvTranspose2D backward w.r.t. input is a regular Conv2D of gradOutput with kernel\n    // gradInput[n,ic,iy,ix] = sum_oc,ky,kx gradOutput[n,oc, iy*s+ky-p, ix*s+kx-p] * kernel[ic,oc,ky,kx]\n    // But we need to account for stride: only positions where (out_pos - pad + ky) is divisible by stride contribute\n    var acc: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var oc: u32 = 0u; oc < params.out_channels; oc = oc + 1u) {\n        for (var ky: u32 = 0u; ky < params.kernel_height; ky = ky + 1u) {\n            for (var kx: u32 = 0u; kx < params.kernel_width; kx = kx + 1u) {\n                // For transposed conv backward: gradInput is the input of the forward pass\n                // forward: output[oy] = sum input[iy] * kernel where oy = iy*stride - pad + ky\n                // backward: gradInput[iy] = sum gradOutput[oy] * kernel where oy = iy*stride - pad + ky\n                let out_y = i32(in_y * params.stride_h) - i32(params.pad_h) + i32(ky);\n                let out_x = i32(in_x * params.stride_w) - i32(params.pad_w) + i32(kx);\n                if (out_y >= 0 && out_y < i32(params.out_height) && out_x >= 0 && out_x < i32(params.out_width)) {\n                    let go_idx = batch * params.out_channels * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                               + oc * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                               + u32(out_y) * params.out_width + u32(out_x);\n                    // kernel layout for transposed conv: [inChannels, outChannels, kH, kW]\n                    let k_idx = ic * params.out_channels * params.kernel_height * params.kernel_width\n                              + oc * params.kernel_height * params.kernel_width\n                              + ky * params.kernel_width + kx;\n                    acc = acc + grad_output[go_idx] * kernel[k_idx];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let gi_idx = batch * params.in_channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n               + ic * params.in_height * params.in_width\n               + in_y * params.in_width + in_x;\n    grad_input[gi_idx] = acc;\n}\n" -> string!

n < params.batch_size; n = n + 1u) {\n        for (var iy: u32 = 0u; iy < params.in_height; iy = iy + 1u) {\n            for (var ix: u32 = 0u; ix < params.in_width; ix = ix + 1u) {\n                let oy = i32(iy * params.stride_h) - i32(params.pad_h) + i32(kh);\n                let ox = i32(ix * params.stride_w) - i32(params.pad_w) + i32(kw);\n                if (oy >= 0 && oy < i32(params.out_height) && ox >= 0 && ox < i32(params.out_width)) {\n                    let in_idx = n * params.in_channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                               + ic * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                               + iy * params.in_width + ix;\n                    let go_idx = n * params.out_channels * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                               + oc * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                               + u32(oy) * params.out_width + u32(ox);\n                    acc = acc + input[in_idx] * grad_output[go_idx];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    grad_kernel[idx] = acc;\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.CrossEntropyForwardSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> predictions: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> targets: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct CEFwdParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    num_classes: u32,\n    _pad1: u32,\n    _pad2: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: CEFwdParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn cross_entropy_forward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let b = gid.x;\n    if (b >= params.batch_size) { return; }\n    let target_i = bitcast<i32>(targets[b]);\n    if (target_i < 0 || u32(target_i) >= params.num_classes) {\n        output[b] = wgsl_f32_bits(0x7fc00000u);\n        return;\n    }\n    let tgt = u32(target_i);\n    let base = b * params.num_classes;\n    var max_val: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    for (var c: u32 = 0u; c < params.num_classes; c = c + 1u) {\n        max_val = max(max_val, predictions[base + c]);\n    }\n    var sum_exp: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var c: u32 = 0u; c < params.num_classes; c = c + 1u) {\n        sum_exp = sum_exp + exp(predictions[base + c] - max_val);\n    }\n    output[b] = -(predictions[base + tgt] - max_val - log(sum_exp));\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.CrossEntropySource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> predictions: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> targets: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    num_classes: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn cross_entropy_loss(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let b = gid.x;\n    if (b >= params.batch_size) { return; }\n    let base = b * params.num_classes;\n    // Stable softmax then cross-entropy\n    var max_val: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    for (var c: u32 = 0u; c < params.num_classes; c = c + 1u) {\n        max_val = max(max_val, predictions[base + c]);\n    }\n    var sum_exp: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var c: u32 = 0u; c < params.num_classes; c = c + 1u) {\n        sum_exp = sum_exp + exp(predictions[base + c] - max_val);\n    }\n    let log_sum = log(sum_exp) + max_val;\n    var loss: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var c: u32 = 0u; c < params.num_classes; c = c + 1u) {\n        loss = loss - targets[base + c] * (predictions[base + c] - log_sum);\n    }\n    output[b] = loss;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn cross_entropy_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.num_classes;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let b = idx / params.num_classes;\n    let c = idx % params.num_classes;\n    let base = b * params.num_classes;\n    // Softmax of predictions\n    var max_val: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.num_classes; i = i + 1u) {\n        max_val = max(max_val, predictions[base + i]);\n    }\n    var sum_exp: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.num_classes; i = i + 1u) {\n        sum_exp = sum_exp + exp(predictions[base + i] - max_val);\n    }\n    let softmax_val = exp(predictions[idx] - max_val) / sum_exp;\n    let inv_batch = 1.0 / f32(params.batch_size);\n    output[idx] = (softmax_val - targets[idx]) * inv_batch;\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.CsrSegmentedSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> col_indices: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> row_ptrs: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct SegParams {\n    M: u32,\n    K: u32,\n    N: u32,\n    epsilon: f32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> params: SegParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn csr_segmented_max(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.M * params.N;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let row = idx / params.N;\n    let j = idx % params.N;\n    let start = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(row_ptrs[row]));\n    let end = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(row_ptrs[row + 1u]));\n    if (start >= end) { output[idx] = 0.0; return; }\n    var max_val: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    for (var i: u32 = start; i < end; i = i + 1u) {\n        let col = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(col_indices[i]));\n        max_val = max(max_val, input[col * params.N + j]);\n    }\n    output[idx] = max_val;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn csr_segmented_min(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.M * params.N;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let row = idx / params.N;\n    let j = idx % params.N;\n    let start = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(row_ptrs[row]));\n    let end = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(row_ptrs[row + 1u]));\n    if (start >= end) { output[idx] = 0.0; return; }\n    var min_val: f32 = 3.402823e+38;\n    for (var i: u32 = start; i < end; i = i + 1u) {\n        let col = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(col_indices[i]));\n        min_val = min(min_val, input[col * params.N + j]);\n    }\n    output[idx] = min_val;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn csr_segmented_stddev(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.M * params.N;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let row = idx / params.N;\n    let j = idx % params.N;\n    let start = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(row_ptrs[row]));\n    let end = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(row_ptrs[row + 1u]));\n    let count = end - start;\n    if (count == 0u) { output[idx] = 0.0; return; }\n    var mean_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var i: u32 = start; i < end; i = i + 1u) {\n        let col = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(col_indices[i]));\n        mean_val = mean_val + input[col * params.N + j];\n    }\n    mean_val = mean_val / f32(count);\n    var var_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var i: u32 = start; i < end; i = i + 1u) {\n        let col = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(col_indices[i]));\n        let d = input[col * params.N + j] - mean_val;\n        var_val = var_val + d * d;\n    }\n    output[idx] = sqrt(var_val / f32(count) + params.epsilon);\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.DeformableConv2DSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> weights: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> offsets: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> mask: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct DeformConvParams {\n    batch_size: u32, in_channels: u32, out_channels: u32,\n    in_height: u32, in_width: u32, out_height: u32, out_width: u32,\n    kernel_height: u32, kernel_width: u32,\n    stride_h: u32, stride_w: u32, pad_h: u32, pad_w: u32,\n    dilation_h: u32, dilation_w: u32,\n    groups: u32, deform_groups: u32, has_mask: u32,\n    _pad0: u32, _pad1: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(5) var<uniform> params: DeformConvParams;\n\nfn bilinear_sample(n: u32, c: u32, y: f32, x: f32) -> f32 {\n    let h = i32(params.in_height);\n    let w = i32(params.in_width);\n    let y0 = i32(floor(y));\n    let x0 = i32(floor(x));\n    let y1 = y0 + 1;\n    let x1 = x0 + 1;\n    let ly = y - f32(y0);\n    let lx = x - f32(x0);\n    let hy = 1.0 - ly;\n    let hx = 1.0 - lx;\n    var val: f32 = 0.0;\n    let base = n * params.in_channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n             + c * params.in_height * params.in_width;\n    if (y0 >= 0 && y0 < h && x0 >= 0 && x0 < w) { val = val + hy * hx * input[base + u32(y0) * params.in_width + u32(x0)]; const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.DIoULossBackward = "\nstruct Params { numBoxes: u32, }\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> goB: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> pred: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> targ: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> gp: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> params: Params;\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let i = gid.x;\n    if (i >= params.numBoxes) { return; let dI1=hi*select(0.0,-1.0,py1>ty1)*iw;\n    let dI2=hi*select(0.0,1.0,px2<tx2)*ih; let dI3=hi*select(0.0,1.0,py2<ty2)*iw;\n    let dU0=-ph-dI0; let dU1=-pw-dI1; let dU2=ph-dI2; let dU3=pw-dI3;\n    let uSq=uA*uA;\n    let ig0=-(dI0*uA-iA*dU0)/uSq; let ig1=-(dI1*uA-iA*dU1)/uSq;\n    let ig2=-(dI2*uA-iA*dU2)/uSq; let ig3=-(dI3*uA-iA*dU3)/uSq;\n    let dx=0.5*(px1+px2)-0.5*(tx1+tx2); let dy=0.5*(py1+py2)-0.5*(ty1+ty2);\n    let rhoSq=dx*dx+dy*dy;\n    let encDx=max(px2,tx2)-min(px1,tx1); let dCSq3=2.0*encDy*select(0.0,1.0,py2>ty2);\n    let dp0=(dx*cSq-rhoSq*dCSq0)/cSqSq; let dp1=(dy*cSq-rhoSq*dCSq1)/cSqSq;\n    let dp2=(dx*cSq-rhoSq*dCSq2)/cSqSq; let dp3=(dy*cSq-rhoSq*dCSq3)/cSqSq;\n    gp[o]=goB[i]*(-ig0+dp0); gp[o+1u]=goB[i]*(-ig1+dp1);\n    gp[o+2u]=goB[i]*(-ig2+dp2); gp[o+3u]=goB[i]*(-ig3+dp3);\n}" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.DotProductSource = "\n// Dot product with workgroup reduction\n// Computes partial sums per workgroup, then a second dispatch reduces them.\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> dp_a: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> dp_b: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> dp_partials: array<f32>;\n\nstruct DpParams {\n    size: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> dp_params: DpParams;\n\nvar<workgroup> dp_shared: array<f32, 256>;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn dot_product(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>,\n               @builtin(local_invocation_id) lid: vec3<u32>,\n               @builtin(workgroup_id) wid: vec3<u32>,\n               @builtin(num_workgroups) nwg: vec3<u32>) {\n    var sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    let grid_size = nwg.x * 256u;\n    var i = gid.x;\n    while (i < dp_params.size) {\n        sum = sum + dp_a[i] * dp_b[i];\n        i = i + grid_size;\n    }\n    dp_shared[lid.x] = sum;\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    for (var stride: u32 = 128u; stride > 0u; stride = stride >> 1u) {\n        if (lid.x < stride) {\n            dp_shared[lid.x] = dp_shared[lid.x] + dp_shared[lid.x + stride];\n        }\n        workgroupBarrier();\n    }\n\n    if (lid.x == 0u) {\n        dp_partials[wid.x] = dp_shared[0];\n    }\n}\n\n// Final reduction of partial sums\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> rp_partials: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> rp_result: array<f32>;\n\nstruct RpParams {\n    count: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> rp_params: RpParams;\n\nvar<workgroup> rp_shared: array<f32, 256>;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn reduce_partial_sums(@builtin(local_invocation_id) lid: vec3<u32>) {\n    var sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    var i = lid.x;\n    while (i < rp_params.count) {\n        sum = sum + rp_partials[i];\n        i = i + 256u;\n    }\n    rp_shared[lid.x] = sum;\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    for (var stride: u32 = 128u; const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.FlashDecodeReduceSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> partialM: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> partialL: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> partialAcc: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> outbuf: array<f32>;\nstruct P { heads: i32, headDim: i32, splits: i32 };\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> p: P;\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let h = i32(gid.x);\n    if (h >= p.heads) { return; }\n    var m = -3.4e38;\n    for (var s = 0; s < p.splits; s = s + 1) {\n        let ms = partialM[h * p.splits + s];\n        if (ms > m) { m = ms; }\n    }\n    var acc: array<f32, 256>;\n    for (var d = 0; d < p.headDim; d = d + 1) { acc[d] = 0.0; }\n    var l = 0.0;\n    for (var s = 0; s < p.splits; s = s + 1) {\n        let idx = h * p.splits + s;\n        let ls = partialL[idx];\n        if (ls <= 0.0) { continue; }\n        let w = exp(partialM[idx] - m);\n        l = l + ls * w;\n        let accBase = idx * p.headDim;\n        for (var d = 0; d < p.headDim; d = d + 1) { acc[d] = acc[d] + partialAcc[accBase + d] * w; }\n    }\n    let inv = select(0.0, 1.0 / l, l > 0.0);\n    for (var d = 0; d < p.headDim; d = d + 1) { outbuf[h * p.headDim + d] = acc[d] * inv; }\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.Fp16ConvertSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct ConvertParams {\n    size: u32,\n    direction: u32, // 0 = to_fp16 (truncate), 1 = from_fp16 (passthrough)\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: ConvertParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn fp16_convert(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= params.size) { return; }\n    let val = input[idx];\n    if (params.direction == 0u) {\n        // FP32 -> FP16 emulation: truncate mantissa to 10 bits\n        let bits = bitcast<u32>(val);\n        let sign = (bits >> 31u) & 1u;\n        let exp_bits = (bits >> 23u) & 0xFFu;\n        let mantissa = bits & 0x7FFFFFu;\n        // Handle special cases\n        if (exp_bits == 0xFFu) {\n            // Inf/NaN: preserve\n            output[idx] = val;\n            return;\n        }\n        let fp16_exp_bias: i32 = 15;\n        let fp32_exp_bias: i32 = 127;\n        let exp_val: i32 = i32(exp_bits) - fp32_exp_bias;\n        if (exp_val < -14) {\n            // Underflow to zero (subnormal in FP16 range)\n            output[idx] = select(-0.0, 0.0, sign == 0u);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (exp_val > 15) {\n            // Overflow to infinity\n            let inf_bits = (sign << 31u) | 0x7F800000u;\n            output[idx] = bitcast<f32>(inf_bits);\n            return;\n        }\n        // Truncate mantissa: keep top 10 bits of 23-bit mantissa\n        let truncated_mantissa = mantissa & 0x7FE000u; // mask off bottom 13 bits\n        let result_bits = (sign << 31u) | (exp_bits << 23u) | truncated_mantissa;\n        output[idx] = bitcast<f32>(result_bits);\n    } else {\n        // FP16 -> FP32: data is already in FP32 with truncated precision, passthrough\n        output[idx] = val;\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

stride > 0u; stride = stride >> 1u) {\n        if (local_idx < stride) { local_data[local_idx] = local_data[local_idx] + local_data[local_idx + stride]; }\n        workgroupBarrier();\n    }\n    if (local_idx == 0u) {\n        shared_var = local_data[0] / f32(params.feature_size);\n        mean_out[batch_idx] = shared_mean;\n        var_out[batch_idx] = shared_var;\n    }\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    let inv_std = 1.0 / sqrt(shared_var + params.epsilon);\n    for (var i: u32 = local_idx; i < params.feature_size; i = i + 256u) {\n        let normalized = (input[base + i] - shared_mean) * inv_std;\n        output[base + i] = gamma[i] * normalized + beta[i];\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.FusedGemmBiasSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> A: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> B: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> bias: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct FusedParams {\n    M: u32,\n    N: u32,\n    K: u32,\n    activation: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> params: FusedParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn gemm_bias_act(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.M * params.N;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let row = idx / params.N;\n    let col = idx % params.N;\n    var acc: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var k: u32 = 0u; k < params.K; k = k + 1u) {\n        acc = acc + A[row * params.K + k] * B[k * params.N + col];\n    }\n    var val = acc + bias[col];\n    if (params.activation == 1u) {\n        val = max(0.0, val);\n    } else if (params.activation == 2u) {\n        val = 0.5 * val * (1.0 + tanh(0.7978845608 * (val + 0.044715 * val * val * val)));\n    } else if (params.activation == 3u) {\n        val = 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-val));\n    } else if (params.activation == 4u) {\n        val = tanh(val);\n    }\n    output[idx] = val;\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.FusedLinearBiasGrad = "\nstruct Params { batchSize: u32, outFeatures: u32, activationType: u32, }\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> go: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> saved: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> gb: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\nfn applyBwBias(g: f32, sv: f32, act: u32) -> f32 {\n    if (act == 0u) { return select(0.0, g, sv > 0.0); }\n    if (act == 1u) { return g * sv * (1.0 - sv); }\n    if (act == 2u) { return g * (1.0 - sv * sv); }\n    if (act == 3u) {\n        let t = tanh(0.7978845608 * (sv + 0.044715 * sv * sv * sv));\n        let dt = 0.7978845608 * (1.0 + 3.0 * 0.044715 * sv * sv) * (1.0 - t * t);\n        return g * (0.5 * (1.0 + t) + 0.5 * sv * dt);\n    }\n    if (act == 4u) { let sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-sv)); return g * (sig + sv * sig * (1.0 - sig)); }\n    return g;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let j = gid.x;\n    if (j >= params.outFeatures) { return; }\n    var s: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var b: u32 = 0u; b < params.batchSize; b++) {\n        s += applyBwBias(go[b * params.outFeatures + j], saved[b * params.outFeatures + j], params.activationType);\n    }\n    gb[j] = s;\n}" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.FusedLinearWeightGrad = "\nstruct Params { batchSize: u32, inFeatures: u32, outFeatures: u32, activationType: u32, }\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> go: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> inp: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> saved: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> gw: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\nfn applyBw(g: f32, sv: f32, act: u32) -> f32 {\n    if (act == 0u) { return select(0.0, g, sv > 0.0); }\n    if (act == 1u) { return g * sv * (1.0 - sv); }\n    if (act == 2u) { return g * (1.0 - sv * sv); }\n    if (act == 3u) {\n        let t = tanh(0.7978845608 * (sv + 0.044715 * sv * sv * sv));\n        let dt = 0.7978845608 * (1.0 + 3.0 * 0.044715 * sv * sv) * (1.0 - t * t);\n        return g * (0.5 * (1.0 + t) + 0.5 * sv * dt);\n    }\n    if (act == 4u) { let sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-sv)); return g * (sig + sv * sig * (1.0 - sig)); }\n    return g;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.inFeatures * params.outFeatures;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let i = idx / params.outFeatures; let j = idx % params.outFeatures;\n    var s: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var b: u32 = 0u; b < params.batchSize; b++) {\n        let masked = applyBw(go[b * params.outFeatures + j], saved[b * params.outFeatures + j], params.activationType);\n        s += inp[b * params.inFeatures + i] * masked;\n    }\n    gw[idx] = s;\n}" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.GatedActivationSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> ga_input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> ga_output: array<f32>;\nstruct GaParams { outer_size: u32, half_dim: u32 }\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> ga_params: GaParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn glu_forward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3u) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = ga_params.outer_size * ga_params.half_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let outer = idx / ga_params.half_dim; let d = idx % ga_params.half_dim;\n    let full_dim = ga_params.half_dim * 2u;\n    let value = ga_input[outer * full_dim + d];\n    let gate = ga_input[outer * full_dim + ga_params.half_dim + d];\n    ga_output[idx] = value * (1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-gate)));\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn geglu_forward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3u) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = ga_params.outer_size * ga_params.half_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let outer = idx / ga_params.half_dim; let d = idx % ga_params.half_dim;\n    let full_dim = ga_params.half_dim * 2u;\n    let value = ga_input[outer * full_dim + d];\n    let gate = ga_input[outer * full_dim + ga_params.half_dim + d];\n    ga_output[idx] = max(value, 0.0) * gate;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn swiglu_forward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3u) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = ga_params.outer_size * ga_params.half_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let outer = idx / ga_params.half_dim; let d = idx % ga_params.half_dim;\n    let full_dim = ga_params.half_dim * 2u;\n    let value = ga_input[outer * full_dim + d];\n    let gate = ga_input[outer * full_dim + ga_params.half_dim + d];\n    let sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-value));\n    ga_output[idx] = value * sig * gate;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn relu_derivative(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3u) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= arrayLength(&ga_input)) { return; }\n    ga_output[idx] = select(0.0, 1.0, ga_input[idx] > 0.0);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sigmoid_derivative(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3u) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= arrayLength(&ga_input)) { return; }\n    let s = ga_input[idx];\n    ga_output[idx] = s * (1.0 - s);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn tanh_derivative(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3u) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= arrayLength(&ga_input)) { return; }\n    let t = ga_input[idx];\n    ga_output[idx] = 1.0 - t * t;\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.GIoULossBackward = "\nstruct Params { numBoxes: u32, }\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> goB: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> pred: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> targ: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> gp: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> params: Params;\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let i = gid.x;\n    if (i >= params.numBoxes) { return; }\n    let o = i * 4u;\n    let px1=pred[o]; let py1=pred[o+1u]; let px2=pred[o+2u]; let py2=pred[o+3u];\n    let tx1=targ[o]; let ty1=targ[o+1u]; let tx2=targ[o+2u]; let ty2=targ[o+3u];\n    let iw=max(0.0,min(px2,tx2)-max(px1,tx1)); let ih=max(0.0,min(py2,ty2)-max(py1,ty1));\n    let iA=iw*ih; let pw=px2-px1; let ph=py2-py1;\n    let uA=pw*ph+(tx2-tx1)*(ty2-ty1)-iA+1e-7;\n    let hi=select(0.0,1.0,iw>0.0&&ih>0.0);\n    let dI0=hi*select(0.0,-1.0,px1>tx1)*ih; let dI1=hi*select(0.0,-1.0,py1>ty1)*iw;\n    let dI2=hi*select(0.0,1.0,px2<tx2)*ih; let dI3=hi*select(0.0,1.0,py2<ty2)*iw;\n    let dU0=-ph-dI0; let dU1=-pw-dI1; let dU2=ph-dI2; let dU3=pw-dI3;\n    let uSq=uA*uA;\n    let ig0=-(dI0*uA-iA*dU0)/uSq; let ig1=-(dI1*uA-iA*dU1)/uSq;\n    let ig2=-(dI2*uA-iA*dU2)/uSq; let ig3=-(dI3*uA-iA*dU3)/uSq;\n    let encW=max(px2,tx2)-min(px1,tx1); let encH=max(py2,ty2)-min(py1,ty1);\n    let encA=encW*encH+1e-7; let encASq=encA*encA;\n    let dEncA0=select(0.0,-1.0,px1<tx1)*encH; let dEncA1=select(0.0,-1.0,py1<ty1)*encW;\n    let dEncA2=select(0.0,1.0,px2>tx2)*encH; let dEncA3=select(0.0,1.0,py2>ty2)*encW;\n    let dP0=-(dU0*encA-uA*dEncA0)/encASq; let dP1=-(dU1*encA-uA*dEncA1)/encASq;\n    let dP2=-(dU2*encA-uA*dEncA2)/encASq; let dP3=-(dU3*encA-uA*dEncA3)/encASq;\n    gp[o]=goB[i]*(-ig0+dP0); gp[o+1u]=goB[i]*(-ig1+dP1);\n    gp[o+2u]=goB[i]*(-ig2+dP2); gp[o+3u]=goB[i]*(-ig3+dP3);\n}" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.GIoULossWgsl = "\nstruct Params { numBoxes: u32, }\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> pred: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> targ: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> loss: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: Params;\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let i = gid.x;\n    if (i >= params.numBoxes) { return; }\n    let o = i * 4u;\n    let px1=pred[o]; let py1=pred[o+1u]; let px2=pred[o+2u]; let py2=pred[o+3u];\n    let tx1=targ[o]; let ty1=targ[o+1u]; let tx2=targ[o+2u]; let ty2=targ[o+3u];\n    let iW=max(0.0,min(px2,tx2)-max(px1,tx1)); let iH=max(0.0,min(py2,ty2)-max(py1,ty1));\n    let iA=iW*iH; let pA=max(0.0,px2-px1)*max(0.0,py2-py1); let tA=max(0.0,tx2-tx1)*max(0.0,ty2-ty1);\n    let uA=pA+tA-iA+1e-7; let iou=iA/uA;\n    let eA=(max(px2,tx2)-min(px1,tx1))*(max(py2,ty2)-min(py1,ty1))+1e-7;\n    loss[i] = 1.0 - (iou - (eA - uA) / eA);\n}" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.GlobalPoolSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    outer_size: u32,\n    spatial_size: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn global_avg_pool_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.outer_size * params.spatial_size;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let outer = idx / params.spatial_size;\n    let inv_spatial = 1.0 / f32(params.spatial_size);\n    output[idx] = input[outer] * inv_spatial;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn global_max_pool_with_indices(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let outer = gid.x;\n    if (outer >= params.outer_size) { return; }\n    let base = outer * params.spatial_size;\n    var max_val: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    var max_idx: u32 = 0u;\n    for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.spatial_size; i = i + 1u) {\n        if (input[base + i] > max_val) {\n            max_val = input[base + i];\n            max_idx = i;\n        }\n    }\n    output[outer * 2u] = max_val;\n    output[outer * 2u + 1u] = bitcast<f32>(i32(max_idx));\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn global_max_pool_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.outer_size * params.spatial_size;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let outer = idx / params.spatial_size;\n    let inner = idx % params.spatial_size;\n    let max_idx = bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(input[outer * 2u + 1u]));\n    if (inner == max_idx) {\n        output[idx] = input[outer * 2u];\n    } else {\n        output[idx] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
