d < params.ndim; d = d + 1u) {\n        in_idx = in_idx + coords[d] * in_strides[perm[d]];\n    }\n    output[idx] = input[in_idx];\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.PhiloxRngSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct RngParams {\n    size: u32,\n    seed_lo: u32,\n    seed_hi: u32,\n    mode: u32,     // 0=uniform, 1=normal, 2=bernoulli_mask\n    min_val: f32,\n    max_val: f32,  // For bernoulli: 1-dropout_rate threshold\n    mean: f32,\n    std_dev: f32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<uniform> params: RngParams;\n\nfn mulhi(a: u32, b: u32) -> u32 {\n    // 32x32->64 multiply high using 16-bit halves: (aH*bH)<<32 + (aH*bL + aL*bH)<<16 + aL*bL\n    let aL = a & 0xFFFFu;\n    let aH = a >> 16u;\n    let bL = b & 0xFFFFu;\n    let bH = b >> 16u;\n    let ll = aL * bL;\n    let lh = aL * bH;\n    let hl = aH * bL;\n    let hh = aH * bH;\n    let mid = (ll >> 16u) + (lh & 0xFFFFu) + (hl & 0xFFFFu);\n    return hh + (lh >> 16u) + (hl >> 16u) + (mid >> 16u);\n}\n\nfn philox_round(ctr0: u32, ctr1: u32, key0: u32, key1: u32) -> vec4<u32> {\n    let M0: u32 = 0xD2511F53u;\n    let M1: u32 = 0xCD9E8D57u;\n    let lo0 = ctr0 * M0;\n    let hi0 = mulhi(ctr0, M0);\n    let lo1 = ctr1 * M1;\n    let hi1 = mulhi(ctr1, M1);\n    return vec4<u32>(hi1 ^ key0, lo1, hi0 ^ key1, lo0);\n}\n\nfn philox4x32(counter: vec4<u32>, key: vec2<u32>) -> vec4<u32> {\n    var c = counter;\n    var k = key;\n    for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < 10u; i = i + 1u) {\n        let r = philox_round(c.x, c.z, k.x, k.y);\n        c = r;\n        k.x = k.x + 0x9E3779B9u;\n        k.y = k.y + 0xBB67AE85u;\n    }\n    return c;\n}\n\nfn u32_to_f32_01(x: u32) -> f32 {\n    return f32(x >> 8u) / 16777216.0;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn gpu_random(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= params.size) { return; }\n    let block = idx / 4u;\n    let lane = idx % 4u;\n    let counter = vec4<u32>(block, 0u, 0u, 0u);\n    let key = vec2<u32>(params.seed_lo, params.seed_hi);\n    let rand = philox4x32(counter, key);\n    let u = u32_to_f32_01(rand[lane]);\n    if (params.mode == 0u) {\n        let seed = params.seed_lo ^ params.seed_hi;\n        let state = idx * 747796405u + seed + 2891336453u;\n        let word = ((state >> ((state >> 28u) + 4u)) ^ state) * 277803737u;\n        let sample = (word >> 22u) ^ word;\n        let uniform = f32(sample >> 8u) * (1.0 / 16777216.0);\n        output[idx] = fma(uniform, params.max_val - params.min_val, params.min_val);\n    } else if (params.mode == 1u) {\n        // Normal via Box-Muller (use pairs)\n        let pair_idx = idx / 2u;\n        let pair_block = pair_idx / 2u;\n        let pair_lane = pair_idx % 2u;\n        let pc = vec4<u32>(pair_block, 1u, 0u, 0u);\n        let pr = philox4x32(pc, key);\n        let u1 = max(u32_to_f32_01(pr[pair_lane * 2u]), 1e-10);\n        let u2 = u32_to_f32_01(pr[pair_lane * 2u + 1u]);\n        let mag = params.std_dev * sqrt(-2.0 * log(u1));\n        if (idx % 2u == 0u) {\n            output[idx] = mag * cos(6.283185307 * u2) + params.mean;\n        } else {\n            output[idx] = mag * sin(6.283185307 * u2) + params.mean;\n        }\n    } else {\n        // Bernoulli mask: 1 if u >= threshold, else 0\n        output[idx] = select(0.0, 1.0, u >= params.max_val);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.PixelShuffleSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\nstruct Params { batch: u32, channels: u32, in_h: u32, in_w: u32, scale: u32, mode: u32, total: u32, _pad: u32 }\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn pixel_shuffle(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= params.total) { return; }\n    let out_h = params.in_h * params.scale;\n    let out_w = params.in_w * params.scale;\n    let ow = idx % out_w;\n    var temp = idx / out_w;\n    let oh = temp % out_h;\n    temp = temp / out_h;\n    let oc = temp % params.channels;\n    let batch_idx = temp / params.channels;\n    let src_c = oc * params.scale * params.scale + (oh % params.scale) * params.scale + (ow % params.scale);\n    output[idx] = input[((batch_idx * params.channels * params.scale * params.scale + src_c) * params.in_h + oh / params.scale) * params.in_w + ow / params.scale];\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn pixel_shuffle_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx >= params.total) { return; }\n    let input_channels = params.channels * params.scale * params.scale;\n    let iw = idx % params.in_w;\n    var temp = idx / params.in_w;\n    let ih = temp % params.in_h;\n    temp = temp / params.in_h;\n    let ic = temp % input_channels;\n    let batch_idx = temp / input_channels;\n    let oc = ic / (params.scale * params.scale);\n    let sub = ic % (params.scale * params.scale);\n    let out_h = params.in_h * params.scale;\n    let out_w = params.in_w * params.scale;\n    output[idx] = input[((batch_idx * params.channels + oc) * out_h + ih * params.scale + sub / params.scale) * out_w + iw * params.scale + sub % params.scale];\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.PoincareExpMapSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> base_pt: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> tangent: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct PEMParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    dim: u32,\n    curvature: f32,\n    _pad: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: PEMParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn poincare_exp_map(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let b = gid.x;\n    if (b >= params.batch_size) { return; }\n    let off = b * params.dim;\n    var bp_sq: f32 = 0.0;\n    var t_norm: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var d: u32 = 0u; d < params.dim; d = d + 1u) {\n        bp_sq = bp_sq + base_pt[off + d] * base_pt[off + d];\n        t_norm = t_norm + tangent[off + d] * tangent[off + d];\n    }\n    t_norm = sqrt(t_norm);\n    let conformal = 2.0 / (1.0 - params.curvature * bp_sq + 1e-10);\n    let th = tanh(conformal * t_norm / 2.0);\n    let scale = select(th / (t_norm * sqrt(params.curvature)), 0.0, t_norm <= 1e-10);\n    var s_sq: f32 = 0.0;\n    var bs: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var d: u32 = 0u; d < params.dim; d = d + 1u) {\n        let sd = tangent[off + d] * scale;\n        s_sq = s_sq + sd * sd;\n        bs = bs + base_pt[off + d] * sd;\n    }\n    let den = max(1.0 + 2.0 * params.curvature * bs + params.curvature * params.curvature * bp_sq * s_sq, 1e-10);\n    for (var d: u32 = 0u; d < params.dim; d = d + 1u) {\n        let sd = tangent[off + d] * scale;\n        output[off + d] = ((1.0 + 2.0 * params.curvature * bs + params.curvature * s_sq) * base_pt[off + d]\n                          + (1.0 - params.curvature * bp_sq) * sd) / den;\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.Pool1DSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> pool_in: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> pool_out: array<f32>;\n\nstruct PoolParams { batch: u32, channels: u32, inLength: u32, outLength: u32, kernelSize: u32, stride: u32, pad0: u32, pad1: u32 };\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> pool_params: PoolParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn avg_pool1d(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = pool_params.batch * pool_params.channels * pool_params.outLength;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let o = idx % pool_params.outLength;\n    let c = (idx / pool_params.outLength) % pool_params.channels;\n    let b = idx / (pool_params.outLength * pool_params.channels);\n    let inOff = (b * pool_params.channels + c) * pool_params.inLength;\n    var sum: f32 = 0.0; var cnt: u32 = 0;\n    for (var k: u32 = 0; k < pool_params.kernelSize; k = k + 1) {\n        let pos = o * pool_params.stride + k;\n        if (pos < pool_params.inLength) { sum = sum + pool_in[inOff + pos]; cnt = cnt + 1; }\n    }\n    pool_out[idx] = select(0.0, sum / f32(cnt), cnt > 0);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn max_pool1d(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = pool_params.batch * pool_params.channels * pool_params.outLength;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let o = idx % pool_params.outLength;\n    let c = (idx / pool_params.outLength) % pool_params.channels;\n    let b = idx / (pool_params.outLength * pool_params.channels);\n    let inOff = (b * pool_params.channels + c) * pool_params.inLength;\n    var maxVal: f32 = -3.402823466e+38;\n    for (var k: u32 = 0; k < pool_params.kernelSize; k = k + 1) {\n        let pos = o * pool_params.stride + k;\n        if (pos < pool_params.inLength) { maxVal = max(maxVal, pool_in[inOff + pos]); }\n    }\n    pool_out[idx] = maxVal;\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.PoolBackwardSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> grad_output: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> grad_input: array<f32>;\n\nstruct PoolParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    channels: u32,\n    in_height: u32,\n    in_width: u32,\n    out_height: u32,\n    out_width: u32,\n    kernel_height: u32,\n    kernel_width: u32,\n    stride_h: u32,\n    stride_w: u32,\n    pad_h: u32,\n    pad_w: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: PoolParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)\nfn avg_pool2d_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let in_x = gid.x;\n    let in_y = gid.y;\n    let c = gid.z % params.channels;\n    let batch = gid.z / params.channels;\n\n    if (in_x >= params.in_width || in_y >= params.in_height || batch >= params.batch_size) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    var acc: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var oh: u32 = 0u; oh < params.out_height; oh = oh + 1u) {\n        for (var ow: u32 = 0u; ow < params.out_width; ow = ow + 1u) {\n            let in_y_start = oh * params.stride_h;\n            let in_x_start = ow * params.stride_w;\n            if (in_y >= in_y_start && in_y < in_y_start + params.kernel_height &&\n                in_x >= in_x_start && in_x < in_x_start + params.kernel_width) {\n                let pool_size = f32(params.kernel_height * params.kernel_width);\n                let go_idx = batch * params.channels * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                           + c * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                           + oh * params.out_width + ow;\n                acc = acc + grad_output[go_idx] / pool_size;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let gi_idx = batch * params.channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n               + c * params.in_height * params.in_width\n               + in_y * params.in_width + in_x;\n    grad_input[gi_idx] = acc;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)\nfn max_pool2d_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let out_x = gid.x;\n    let out_y = gid.y;\n    let c = gid.z % params.channels;\n    let batch = gid.z / params.channels;\n\n    if (out_x >= params.out_width || out_y >= params.out_height || batch >= params.batch_size) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Find the max input position for this output position\n    var max_val: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    var max_ih: u32 = 0u;\n    var max_iw: u32 = 0u;\n\n    for (var ky: u32 = 0u; ky < params.kernel_height; ky = ky + 1u) {\n        for (var kx: u32 = 0u; kx < params.kernel_width; kx = kx + 1u) {\n            let in_y = i32(out_y * params.stride_h + ky) - i32(params.pad_h);\n            let in_x = i32(out_x * params.stride_w + kx) - i32(params.pad_w);\n            if (in_y >= 0 && in_y < i32(params.in_height) && in_x >= 0 && in_x < i32(params.in_width)) {\n                let in_idx = batch * params.channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                           + c * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                           + u32(in_y) * params.in_width + u32(in_x);\n                if (input[in_idx] > max_val) {\n                    max_val = input[in_idx];\n                    max_ih = u32(in_y);\n                    max_iw = u32(in_x);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Route gradient to the max position (atomic add not available, use direct write)\n    let go_idx = batch * params.channels * params.out_height * params.out_width\n               + c * params.out_height * params.out_width\n               + out_y * params.out_width + out_x;\n    let gi_idx = batch * params.channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n               + c * params.in_height * params.in_width\n               + max_ih * params.in_width + max_iw;\n    // Note: This has race conditions if multiple output positions map to the same max input.\n    // For production, an atomic add or a different approach would be needed.\n    grad_input[gi_idx] = grad_input[gi_idx] + grad_output[go_idx];\n}\n" -> string!

ky < params.kernel_height; ky = ky + 1u) {\n        for (var kx: u32 = 0u; kx < params.kernel_width; kx = kx + 1u) {\n            let in_y = i32(out_y * params.stride_h + ky) - i32(params.pad_h);\n            let in_x = i32(out_x * params.stride_w + kx) - i32(params.pad_w);\n\n            if (in_y >= 0 && in_y < i32(params.in_height) && in_x >= 0 && in_x < i32(params.in_width)) {\n                let in_idx = batch * params.channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                           + c * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                           + u32(in_y) * params.in_width + u32(in_x);\n                max_val = max(max_val, input[in_idx]);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let out_idx = batch * params.channels * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                + c * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                + out_y * params.out_width + out_x;\n    output[out_idx] = max_val;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)\nfn avg_pool2d(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let out_x = gid.x;\n    let out_y = gid.y;\n    let c = gid.z % params.channels;\n    let batch = gid.z / params.channels;\n\n    if (out_x >= params.out_width || out_y >= params.out_height || batch >= params.batch_size) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    var sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    var count: f32 = 0.0;\n\n    for (var ky: u32 = 0u; ky < params.kernel_height; ky = ky + 1u) {\n        for (var kx: u32 = 0u; kx < params.kernel_width; kx = kx + 1u) {\n            let in_y = i32(out_y * params.stride_h + ky) - i32(params.pad_h);\n            let in_x = i32(out_x * params.stride_w + kx) - i32(params.pad_w);\n\n            if (in_y >= 0 && in_y < i32(params.in_height) && in_x >= 0 && in_x < i32(params.in_width)) {\n                let in_idx = batch * params.channels * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                           + c * params.in_height * params.in_width\n                           + u32(in_y) * params.in_width + u32(in_x);\n                sum = sum + input[in_idx];\n                count = count + 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let out_idx = batch * params.channels * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                + c * params.out_height * params.out_width\n                + out_y * params.out_width + out_x;\n    output[out_idx] = sum / max(count, 1.0);\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.ReductionSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    size: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\nvar<workgroup> shared_data: array<f32, 256>;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sum_reduce(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>,\n              @builtin(local_invocation_id) lid: vec3<u32>,\n              @builtin(workgroup_id) wid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let local_idx = lid.x;\n\n    // Load data to shared memory\n    if (idx < params.size) {\n        shared_data[local_idx] = input[idx];\n    } else {\n        shared_data[local_idx] = 0.0;\n    }\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    // Parallel reduction\n    for (var stride: u32 = 128u; stride > 0u; stride = stride >> 1u) {\n        if (local_idx < stride) {\n            shared_data[local_idx] = shared_data[local_idx] + shared_data[local_idx + stride];\n        }\n        workgroupBarrier();\n    }\n\n    // Write result\n    if (local_idx == 0u) {\n        output[wid.x] = shared_data[0];\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn max_reduce(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>,\n              @builtin(local_invocation_id) lid: vec3<u32>,\n              @builtin(workgroup_id) wid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let local_idx = lid.x;\n\n    if (idx < params.size) {\n        shared_data[local_idx] = input[idx];\n    } else {\n        shared_data[local_idx] = -3.402823e+38; const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.RMSNormSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> gamma: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> save_rms: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    feature_size: u32,\n    epsilon: f32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\nvar<workgroup> shared_rms: f32;\nvar<workgroup> local_data: array<f32, 256>;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn rms_norm(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>,\n            @builtin(local_invocation_id) lid: vec3<u32>,\n            @builtin(workgroup_id) wid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let batch_idx = wid.x;\n    let local_idx = lid.x;\n    let base = batch_idx * params.feature_size;\n\n    // Compute sum of squares\n    var local_sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var i: u32 = local_idx; i < params.feature_size; i = i + 256u) {\n        let v = input[base + i];\n        local_sum = local_sum + v * v;\n    }\n    local_data[local_idx] = local_sum;\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    for (var stride: u32 = 128u; stride > 0u; stride = stride >> 1u) {\n        if (local_idx < stride) {\n            local_data[local_idx] = local_data[local_idx] + local_data[local_idx + stride];\n        }\n        workgroupBarrier();\n    }\n    if (local_idx == 0u) {\n        let rms_val = sqrt(local_data[0] / f32(params.feature_size) + params.epsilon);\n        shared_rms = 1.0 / rms_val;\n        save_rms[batch_idx] = rms_val;\n    }\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    for (var i: u32 = local_idx; i < params.feature_size; i = i + 256u) {\n        output[base + i] = gamma[i] * input[base + i] * shared_rms;\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.RnnCellSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> weights: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> state: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct RnnParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    input_size: u32,\n    hidden_size: u32,\n    is_gru: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> params: RnnParams;\n\nfn sigmoid(x: f32) -> f32 { return 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-x)); }\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn lstm_cell(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.hidden_size;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let b = idx / params.hidden_size;\n    let h = idx % params.hidden_size;\n    let hs = params.hidden_size;\n    let in_s = params.input_size;\n    // Compute 4 gates: i, f, g, o\n    // weights layout: [W_ih (4*hs x in_s), W_hh (4*hs x hs), bias (4*hs)]\n    var gates: array<f32, 4>;\n    for (var gate: u32 = 0u; gate < 4u; gate = gate + 1u) {\n        var val: f32 = 0.0;\n        // Input contribution: W_ih[gate*hs+h, :] * input[b, :]\n        let w_ih_off = (gate * hs + h) * in_s;\n        for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < in_s; j = j + 1u) {\n            val = val + weights[w_ih_off + j] * input[b * in_s + j];\n        }\n        // Hidden contribution: W_hh[gate*hs+h, :] * h_prev[b, :]\n        let w_hh_off = 4u * hs * in_s + (gate * hs + h) * hs;\n        for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < hs; j = j + 1u) {\n            val = val + weights[w_hh_off + j] * output[b * hs + j]; // output holds h_prev\n        }\n        // Bias\n        let bias_off = 4u * hs * in_s + 4u * hs * hs + gate * hs + h;\n        val = val + weights[bias_off];\n        gates[gate] = val;\n    }\n    let i_gate = sigmoid(gates[0]);\n    let f_gate = sigmoid(gates[1]);\n    let g_gate = fast_tanh(gates[2]);\n    let o_gate = sigmoid(gates[3]);\n    // state holds cell state c_prev; update c and h\n    let c_new = f_gate * state[idx] + i_gate * g_gate;\n    let h_new = o_gate * fast_tanh(c_new);\n    state[idx] = c_new;\n    output[idx] = h_new;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn gru_cell(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.hidden_size;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let b = idx / params.hidden_size;\n    let h = idx % params.hidden_size;\n    let hs = params.hidden_size;\n    let in_s = params.input_size;\n    // Compute 3 gates: r(reset), z(update), n(new)\n    var gates: array<f32, 3>;\n    for (var gate: u32 = 0u; gate < 3u; gate = gate + 1u) {\n        var val: f32 = 0.0;\n        let w_ih_off = (gate * hs + h) * in_s;\n        for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < in_s; j = j + 1u) {\n            val = val + weights[w_ih_off + j] * input[b * in_s + j];\n        }\n        let w_hh_off = 3u * hs * in_s + (gate * hs + h) * hs;\n        for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < hs; j = j + 1u) {\n            val = val + weights[w_hh_off + j] * output[b * hs + j];\n        }\n        let bias_off = 3u * hs * in_s + 3u * hs * hs + gate * hs + h;\n        val = val + weights[bias_off];\n        gates[gate] = val;\n    }\n    let r_gate = sigmoid(gates[0]);\n    let z_gate = sigmoid(gates[1]);\n    // For n_gate, recompute hidden contribution with reset gate\n    var n_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    let w_ih_n_off = (2u * hs + h) * in_s;\n    for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < in_s; j = j + 1u) {\n        n_val = n_val + weights[w_ih_n_off + j] * input[b * in_s + j];\n    }\n    var n_hid: f32 = 0.0;\n    let w_hh_n_off = 3u * hs * in_s + (2u * hs + h) * hs;\n    for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < hs; j = j + 1u) {\n        n_hid = n_hid + weights[w_hh_n_off + j] * output[b * hs + j];\n    }\n    let n_gate = fast_tanh(n_val + r_gate * n_hid);\n    output[idx] = (1.0 - z_gate) * n_gate + z_gate * output[idx];\n}\n" -> string!

Integral exponents are\n        // valid, so evaluate |x|^exponent and restore odd parity.\n        if (x < 0.0) {\n            if (exponent == trunc(exponent)) {\n                let magnitude = pow(-x, exponent);\n                let abs_exponent = abs(exponent);\n                let is_odd = (abs_exponent - 2.0 * floor(abs_exponent * 0.5)) == 1.0;\n                B[idx] = select(magnitude, -magnitude, is_odd);\n            } else {\n                B[idx] = wgsl_f32_bits(0x7fc00000u);\n            }\n        } else {\n            B[idx] = pow(x, exponent);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn clamp_scalar(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < params.size) {\n        B[idx] = clamp(A[idx], -params.scalar, params.scalar);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.SoftmaxBackwardSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> grad_output: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> softmax_output: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> grad_input: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    features: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\nvar<workgroup> shared_dot: f32;\nvar<workgroup> local_data: array<f32, 256>;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn softmax_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>,\n                    @builtin(local_invocation_id) lid: vec3<u32>,\n                    @builtin(workgroup_id) wid: vec3<u32>) {\n    // One workgroup per row, folded over a 2-D grid: one dimension holds at most 65535 workgroups.\n    let batch_idx = wid.x + wid.y * 65535u;\n    if (batch_idx >= params.batch_size) { return; }\n    let local_idx = lid.x;\n    let base = batch_idx * params.features;\n\n    // Compute dot product of grad_output and softmax_output\n    var local_dot: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var i: u32 = local_idx; i < params.features; i = i + 256u) {\n        local_dot = local_dot + grad_output[base + i] * softmax_output[base + i];\n    }\n    local_data[local_idx] = local_dot;\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    for (var stride: u32 = 128u; stride > 0u; stride = stride >> 1u) {\n        if (local_idx < stride) {\n            local_data[local_idx] = local_data[local_idx] + local_data[local_idx + stride];\n        }\n        workgroupBarrier();\n    }\n\n    if (local_idx == 0u) {\n        shared_dot = local_data[0];\n    }\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    // grad_input = softmax_output * (grad_output - dot)\n    for (var i: u32 = local_idx; i < params.features; i = i + 256u) {\n        grad_input[base + i] = softmax_output[base + i] * (grad_output[base + i] - shared_dot);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.SoftmaxSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    class_count: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\nvar<workgroup> shared_max: f32;\nvar<workgroup> shared_sum: f32;\nvar<workgroup> local_data: array<f32, 256>;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn softmax(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>,\n           @builtin(local_invocation_id) lid: vec3<u32>,\n           @builtin(workgroup_id) wid: vec3<u32>) {\n    // One workgroup per row, folded over a 2-D grid: one dimension holds at most 65535 workgroups.\n    let batch_idx = wid.x + wid.y * 65535u;\n    if (batch_idx >= params.batch_size) { return; }\n    let local_idx = lid.x;\n    let base = batch_idx * params.class_count;\n\n    // Load and find max\n    var local_max: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n    for (var i: u32 = local_idx; i < params.class_count; i = i + 256u) {\n        local_max = max(local_max, input[base + i]);\n    }\n    local_data[local_idx] = local_max;\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    // Reduce to find global max\n    for (var stride: u32 = 128u; stride > 0u; stride = stride >> 1u) {\n        if (local_idx < stride && local_idx + stride < 256u) {\n            local_data[local_idx] = max(local_data[local_idx], local_data[local_idx + stride]);\n        }\n        workgroupBarrier();\n    }\n\n    if (local_idx == 0u) {\n        shared_max = local_data[0];\n    }\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    // Compute exp and sum\n    var local_sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var i: u32 = local_idx; i < params.class_count; i = i + 256u) {\n        local_sum = local_sum + exp(input[base + i] - shared_max);\n    }\n    local_data[local_idx] = local_sum;\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    for (var stride: u32 = 128u; stride > 0u; stride = stride >> 1u) {\n        if (local_idx < stride && local_idx + stride < 256u) {\n            local_data[local_idx] = local_data[local_idx] + local_data[local_idx + stride];\n        }\n        workgroupBarrier();\n    }\n\n    if (local_idx == 0u) {\n        shared_sum = local_data[0];\n    }\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    // Write normalized output\n    for (var i: u32 = local_idx; i < params.class_count; i = i + 256u) {\n        output[base + i] = exp(input[base + i] - shared_max) / shared_sum;\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.SoftmaxVariantsSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> sv_input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> sv_output: array<f32>;\nstruct SvParams { outer_size: u32, inner_size: u32 }\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> sv_params: SvParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn log_softmax(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3u) {\n    let row = gid.x;\n    if (row >= sv_params.outer_size) { return; }\n    let base = row * sv_params.inner_size;\n    var max_val: f32 = -1e38;\n    for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < sv_params.inner_size; j = j + 1u) { max_val = max(max_val, sv_input[base + j]); }\n    var sum_exp: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < sv_params.inner_size; j = j + 1u) { sum_exp += exp(sv_input[base + j] - max_val); }\n    let log_sum = log(sum_exp);\n    for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < sv_params.inner_size; j = j + 1u) { sv_output[base + j] = sv_input[base + j] - max_val - log_sum; }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn taylor_softmax(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3u) {\n    let row = gid.x;\n    if (row >= sv_params.outer_size) { return; }\n    let base = row * sv_params.inner_size;\n    var sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < sv_params.inner_size; j = j + 1u) {\n        let x = sv_input[base + j]; let t = max(1.0 + x + 0.5 * x * x, 1e-10);\n        sv_output[base + j] = t; sum += t;\n    }\n    let inv = 1.0 / sum;\n    for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < sv_params.inner_size; j = j + 1u) { sv_output[base + j] *= inv; }\n}\n" -> string!

}\n    let row = idx / blocks_per_row;\n    let blk = idx % blocks_per_row;\n    let base_idx = row * params.K + blk * 4u;\n    var vals: array<f32, 4>;\n    var abs_v: array<f32, 4>;\n    var idxs: array<u32, 4> = array<u32, 4>(0u, 1u, 2u, 3u);\n    for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < 4u; i = i + 1u) {\n        vals[i] = A[base_idx + i];\n        abs_v[i] = abs(vals[i]);\n    }\n    for (var sweep: u32 = 0u; sweep < 3u; sweep = sweep + 1u) {\n        for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < 3u - sweep; j = j + 1u) {\n            if (abs_v[j] < abs_v[j + 1u]) {\n                let tv = abs_v[j]; abs_v[j] = abs_v[j + 1u]; abs_v[j + 1u] = tv;\n                let ti = idxs[j]; idxs[j] = idxs[j + 1u]; idxs[j + 1u] = ti;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    let s_off = row * params.sparse_K + blk * 2u;\n    B[s_off] = vals[idxs[0]];\n    B[s_off + 1u] = vals[idxs[1]];\n    C[s_off] = bitcast<f32>(idxs[0]);\n    C[s_off + 1u] = bitcast<f32>(idxs[1]);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn decompress_2x4_sparse(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let blocks_per_row = params.K / 4u;\n    let total_blocks = params.M * blocks_per_row;\n    if (idx >= total_blocks) { return; }\n    let row = idx / blocks_per_row;\n    let blk = idx % blocks_per_row;\n    let s_off = row * params.sparse_K + blk * 2u;\n    let base_idx = row * params.K + blk * 4u;\n    C[base_idx] = 0.0; C[base_idx + 1u] = 0.0;\n    C[base_idx + 2u] = 0.0; const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.SparseOptimizerSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read_write> params_arr: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> indices: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> values: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> state1: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read_write> state2: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(5) var<storage, read_write> state3: array<f32>;\n\nstruct OptimizerParams {\n    size: u32,\n    lr: f32,\n    beta1: f32,\n    beta2: f32,\n    epsilon: f32,\n    weight_decay: f32,\n    t: u32,\n    extra: f32,\n    param_size: u32,\n    _pad0: u32,\n    _pad1: u32,\n    _pad2: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(6) var<uniform> opt_params: OptimizerParams;\n\n// CONTRACT: the sparse index list is expected to be UNIQUE per dispatch - the caller pre-aggregates\n// (sums) gradients for repeated indices before applying them, as is standard for sparse/embedding\n// gradient updates. Under that contract no two invocations touch the same param/state slot, so the\n// non-atomic scatter below is race-free and matches the sequential CPU reference bit-for-bit.\n// (WGSL storage atomics are u32/i32 only, so an f32 accumulate would need a bitcast CAS loop; that is\n// deferred unless a duplicate-index workload is actually required.) The same contract holds for the\n// CUDA/HIP/OpenCL/Metal/Vulkan sparse kernels.\nfn decode_sparse_index(raw: f32) -> u32 {\n    let bitcast = bitcast<i32>(raw);\n    if (bitcast >= 0 && u32(bitcast) < opt_params.param_size) {\n        return u32(bitcast);\n    }\n    // Validate in the FLOAT domain before i32(raw) - the conversion is implementation-defined for NaN/\n    // Inf or out-of-range values. (raw == raw) rejects NaN; the range check rejects +/-Inf.\n    if (raw == raw && raw >= 0.0 && raw < f32(opt_params.param_size) && raw == trunc(raw)) {\n        return u32(raw);\n    }\n    return opt_params.param_size;\n}\n\nfn sparse_slot(k: u32) -> u32 {\n    return decode_sparse_index(indices[k]);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_sgd_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * grad;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_sgd_momentum_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + grad;\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * state1[i];\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_adam_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    let grad = values[k];\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n    state2[i] = opt_params.beta2 * state2[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad * grad;\n    let step = f32(opt_params.t);\n    let m_hat = state1[i] / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, step));\n    let v_hat = state2[i] / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta2, step));\n    var update = opt_params.lr * m_hat / (sqrt(v_hat) + opt_params.epsilon);\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { update = update + opt_params.lr * opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - update;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_adamw_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    let grad = values[k];\n    var p = params_arr[i];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { p = p - opt_params.lr * opt_params.weight_decay * p; }\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n    state2[i] = opt_params.beta2 * state2[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad * grad;\n    let step = f32(opt_params.t);\n    let m_hat = state1[i] / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, step));\n    let v_hat = state2[i] / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta2, step));\n    params_arr[i] = p - opt_params.lr * m_hat / (sqrt(v_hat) + opt_params.epsilon);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_rmsprop_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad * grad;\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * grad / (sqrt(state1[i]) + opt_params.epsilon);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_adagrad_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    state1[i] = state1[i] + grad * grad;\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * grad / (sqrt(state1[i]) + opt_params.epsilon);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_nag_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    let v_old = state1[i];\n    let v_new = opt_params.beta1 * v_old + grad;\n    state1[i] = v_new;\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * ((1.0 + opt_params.beta1) * v_new - opt_params.beta1 * v_old);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_adadelta_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    let one_minus_rho = 1.0 - opt_params.beta1;\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + one_minus_rho * grad * grad;\n    let dx = sqrt(state2[i] + opt_params.epsilon) / sqrt(state1[i] + opt_params.epsilon) * grad;\n    state2[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state2[i] + one_minus_rho * dx * dx;\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - dx;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_amsgrad_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n    state2[i] = opt_params.beta2 * state2[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad * grad;\n    state3[i] = max(state3[i], state2[i]);\n    let step = f32(opt_params.t);\n    let m_hat = state1[i] / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, step));\n    let v_hat = state3[i] / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta2, step));\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * m_hat / (sqrt(v_hat) + opt_params.epsilon);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_adamax_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n    state2[i] = max(opt_params.beta2 * state2[i], abs(grad));\n    let lr_adj = opt_params.lr / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, f32(opt_params.t)));\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - lr_adj * state1[i] / (state2[i] + opt_params.epsilon);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_lion_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    let grad = values[k];\n    var update = sign(opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad);\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { update = update + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * update;\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta2 * state1[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad;\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_nadam_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    var grad = values[k];\n    if (opt_params.weight_decay > 0.0) { grad = grad + opt_params.weight_decay * params_arr[i]; }\n    state1[i] = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad;\n    state2[i] = opt_params.beta2 * state2[i] + (1.0 - opt_params.beta2) * grad * grad;\n    let step = f32(opt_params.t);\n    let bc1 = 1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, step);\n    let bc2 = 1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta2, step);\n    let m_hat = opt_params.beta1 * state1[i] / (1.0 - pow(opt_params.beta1, step + 1.0)) + (1.0 - opt_params.beta1) * grad / bc1;\n    let v_hat = state2[i] / bc2;\n    params_arr[i] = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * m_hat / (sqrt(v_hat) + opt_params.epsilon);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_ftrl_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    let grad = values[k];\n    let n_old = state2[i];\n    let n_new = n_old + grad * grad;\n    state2[i] = n_new;\n    let sigma = (sqrt(n_new) - sqrt(n_old)) / opt_params.lr;\n    state1[i] = state1[i] + grad - sigma * params_arr[i];\n    if (abs(state1[i]) <= opt_params.beta1) {\n        params_arr[i] = 0.0;\n    } else {\n        let sign_z = select(-1.0, 1.0, state1[i] > 0.0);\n        params_arr[i] = (sign_z * opt_params.beta1 - state1[i]) /\n            ((opt_params.extra + sqrt(n_new)) / opt_params.lr + opt_params.beta2);\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn sparse_proximal_l1_update(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let k = gid.x;\n    if (k >= opt_params.size) { return; }\n    let i = sparse_slot(k); if (i >= opt_params.param_size) { return; }\n    let p = params_arr[i] - opt_params.lr * values[k];\n    let threshold = opt_params.lr * opt_params.beta1;\n    if (p > threshold) {\n        params_arr[i] = p - threshold;\n    } else if (p < -threshold) {\n        params_arr[i] = p + threshold;\n    } else {\n        params_arr[i] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.SpatialTransformerSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input_a: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> input_b: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    param1: u32,\n    param2: u32,\n    param3: u32,\n    param4: u32,\n    _pad1: u32,\n    _pad2: u32,\n    _pad3: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn affine_grid(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    // param1 = outHeight, param2 = outWidth\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.param1 * params.param2;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let w = idx % params.param2;\n    let h = (idx / params.param2) % params.param1;\n    let n = idx / (params.param2 * params.param1);\n    let ny = select(2.0 * f32(h) / f32(params.param1 - 1u) - 1.0, 0.0, params.param1 <= 1u);\n    let nx = select(2.0 * f32(w) / f32(params.param2 - 1u) - 1.0, 0.0, params.param2 <= 1u);\n    let off = n * 6u;\n    let g_idx = (n * params.param1 * params.param2 + h * params.param2 + w) * 2u;\n    output[g_idx] = input_a[off] * nx + input_a[off + 1u] * ny + input_a[off + 2u];\n    output[g_idx + 1u] = input_a[off + 3u] * nx + input_a[off + 4u] * ny + input_a[off + 5u];\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn grid_sample(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    // param1 = channels, param2 = inHeight, param3 = inWidth, param4 = outHeight*outWidth\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.param1 * params.param4;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let spatial = idx % params.param4;\n    let c = (idx / params.param4) % params.param1;\n    let n = idx / (params.param4 * params.param1);\n    let g_idx = (n * params.param4 + spatial) * 2u;\n    let sx = (input_b[g_idx] + 1.0) * 0.5 * f32(params.param3 - 1u);\n    let sy = (input_b[g_idx + 1u] + 1.0) * 0.5 * f32(params.param2 - 1u);\n    // #1134: a NaN, infinite or out-of-int-range point samples nothing (the CPU rule); on the bits, as WGSL may assume finite floats.\n    if ((bitcast<u32>(sx) & 0x7FFFFFFFu) >= 0x4EFFFFFFu || (bitcast<u32>(sy) & 0x7FFFFFFFu) >= 0x4EFFFFFFu) { output[idx] = 0.0; return; }\n    let x0 = i32(floor(sx)); var v11: f32 = 0.0;\n    if (x0 >= 0 && x0 < i32(params.param3) && y0 >= 0 && y0 < i32(params.param2)) { v00 = input_a[in_base + u32(y0) * params.param3 + u32(x0)]; }\n    if (x0 + 1 < i32(params.param3) && y0 >= 0 && y0 < i32(params.param2)) { v01 = input_a[in_base + u32(y0) * params.param3 + u32(x0 + 1)]; }\n    if (x0 >= 0 && x0 < i32(params.param3) && y0 + 1 < i32(params.param2)) { v10 = input_a[in_base + u32(y0 + 1) * params.param3 + u32(x0)]; }\n    if (x0 + 1 < i32(params.param3) && y0 + 1 < i32(params.param2)) { v11 = input_a[in_base + u32(y0 + 1) * params.param3 + u32(x0 + 1)]; }\n    output[idx] = (1.0 - fy) * ((1.0 - fx) * v00 + fx * v01) + fy * ((1.0 - fx) * v10 + fx * v11);\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.TopKSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> values: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> indices: array<f32>;\n\nstruct TopKParams {\n    outer_size: u32,\n    reduce_size: u32,\n    k: u32,\n    _pad: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> params: TopKParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn topk_select(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let b = gid.x;\n    if (b >= params.outer_size) { return; }\n    let base = b * params.reduce_size;\n    let out_base = b * params.k;\n    // Selection sort for top-K (optimal for small K)\n    for (var j: u32 = 0u; j < params.k && j < params.reduce_size; j = j + 1u) {\n        var best_val: f32 = -3.402823e+38;\n        var best_idx: u32 = 0u;\n        for (var c: u32 = 0u; c < params.reduce_size; c = c + 1u) {\n            let v = input[base + c];\n            var already_selected = false;\n            for (var prev: u32 = 0u; prev < j; prev = prev + 1u) {\n                if (bitcast<u32>(bitcast<i32>(indices[out_base + prev])) == c) {\n                    already_selected = true;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n            if (!already_selected && v > best_val) {\n                best_val = v;\n                best_idx = c;\n            }\n        }\n        values[out_base + j] = best_val;\n        indices[out_base + j] = bitcast<f32>(best_idx);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.TransposeSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct TransposeParams {\n    rows: u32,\n    cols: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: TransposeParams;\n\nconst TILE_SIZE: u32 = 16u;\nvar<workgroup> tile: array<array<f32, 17>, 16>;  // +1 to avoid bank conflicts\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(16, 16)\nfn transpose(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>,\n             @builtin(local_invocation_id) lid: vec3<u32>,\n             @builtin(workgroup_id) wid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let x = wid.x * TILE_SIZE + lid.x;\n    let y = wid.y * TILE_SIZE + lid.y;\n\n    // Load tile\n    if (x < params.cols && y < params.rows) {\n        tile[lid.y][lid.x] = input[y * params.cols + x];\n    }\n    workgroupBarrier();\n\n    // Write transposed\n    let tx = wid.y * TILE_SIZE + lid.x;\n    let ty = wid.x * TILE_SIZE + lid.y;\n\n    if (tx < params.rows && ty < params.cols) {\n        output[ty * params.rows + tx] = tile[lid.x][lid.y];\n    }\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn transpose_simple(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.rows * params.cols;\n\n    if (idx >= total) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    let row = idx / params.cols;\n    let col = idx % params.cols;\n\n    output[col * params.rows + row] = input[row * params.cols + col];\n}\n" -> string!

const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.TripletContrastiveSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> A: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> B: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> C: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct TCParams {\n    batch_size: u32,\n    embedding_dim: u32,\n    margin: f32,\n    _pad: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> params: TCParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn triplet_loss_elem(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let b = gid.x;\n    if (b >= params.batch_size) { return; }\n    var dp: f32 = 0.0;\n    var dn: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var f: u32 = 0u; f < params.embedding_dim; f = f + 1u) {\n        let da_p = A[b * params.embedding_dim + f] - B[b * params.embedding_dim + f];\n        dp = dp + da_p * da_p;\n        let da_n = A[b * params.embedding_dim + f] - C[b * params.embedding_dim + f];\n        dn = dn + da_n * da_n;\n    }\n    output[b] = max(0.0, sqrt(dp) - sqrt(dn) + params.margin);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn contrastive_loss_elem(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let b = gid.x;\n    if (b >= params.batch_size) { return; }\n    var dist: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var f: u32 = 0u; f < params.embedding_dim; f = f + 1u) {\n        let d = A[b * params.embedding_dim + f] - B[b * params.embedding_dim + f];\n        dist = dist + d * d;\n    }\n    dist = sqrt(dist);\n    let label = C[b];\n    let hinge = max(0.0, params.margin - dist);\n    output[b] = select(hinge * hinge, dist * dist, label > 0.5);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn triplet_loss_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.embedding_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let b = idx / params.embedding_dim;\n    let f = idx % params.embedding_dim;\n    let a_val = A[idx];\n    let p_val = B[idx];\n    let n_val = C[idx];\n    var dp: f32 = 0.0;\n    var dn: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var ff: u32 = 0u; ff < params.embedding_dim; ff = ff + 1u) {\n        let da_p = A[b * params.embedding_dim + ff] - B[b * params.embedding_dim + ff];\n        dp = dp + da_p * da_p;\n        let da_n = A[b * params.embedding_dim + ff] - C[b * params.embedding_dim + ff];\n        dn = dn + da_n * da_n;\n    }\n    let loss = sqrt(dp) - sqrt(dn) + params.margin;\n    if (loss <= 0.0) { output[idx] = 0.0; return; }\n    let inv_dp = select(0.0, 1.0 / sqrt(dp), dp > 1e-10);\n    let inv_dn = select(0.0, 1.0 / sqrt(dn), dn > 1e-10);\n    output[idx] = ((a_val - p_val) * inv_dp - (a_val - n_val) * inv_dn) / f32(params.batch_size);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn triplet_grad_positive(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.embedding_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let b = idx / params.embedding_dim;\n    let a_val = A[idx];\n    let p_val = B[idx];\n    var dp: f32 = 0.0;\n    var dn: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var ff: u32 = 0u; ff < params.embedding_dim; ff = ff + 1u) {\n        let da_p = A[b * params.embedding_dim + ff] - B[b * params.embedding_dim + ff];\n        dp = dp + da_p * da_p;\n        let da_n = A[b * params.embedding_dim + ff] - C[b * params.embedding_dim + ff];\n        dn = dn + da_n * da_n;\n    }\n    let loss = sqrt(dp) - sqrt(dn) + params.margin;\n    if (loss <= 0.0) { output[idx] = 0.0; return; }\n    let inv_dp = select(0.0, 1.0 / sqrt(dp), dp > 1e-10);\n    // gradPositive = -(a - p) / ||a - p|| / batch_size = (p - a) / ||a - p|| / batch_size\n    output[idx] = -(a_val - p_val) * inv_dp / f32(params.batch_size);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn triplet_grad_negative(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.embedding_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let b = idx / params.embedding_dim;\n    let a_val = A[idx];\n    let n_val = C[idx];\n    var dp: f32 = 0.0;\n    var dn: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var ff: u32 = 0u; ff < params.embedding_dim; ff = ff + 1u) {\n        let da_p = A[b * params.embedding_dim + ff] - B[b * params.embedding_dim + ff];\n        dp = dp + da_p * da_p;\n        let da_n = A[b * params.embedding_dim + ff] - C[b * params.embedding_dim + ff];\n        dn = dn + da_n * da_n;\n    }\n    let loss = sqrt(dp) - sqrt(dn) + params.margin;\n    if (loss <= 0.0) { output[idx] = 0.0; return; }\n    let inv_dn = select(0.0, 1.0 / sqrt(dn), dn > 1e-10);\n    // gradNegative = (a - n) / ||a - n|| / batch_size\n    output[idx] = (a_val - n_val) * inv_dn / f32(params.batch_size);\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn contrastive_loss_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_size * params.embedding_dim;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let b = idx / params.embedding_dim;\n    let f = idx % params.embedding_dim;\n    var dist: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var ff: u32 = 0u; ff < params.embedding_dim; ff = ff + 1u) {\n        let d = A[b * params.embedding_dim + ff] - B[b * params.embedding_dim + ff];\n        dist = dist + d * d;\n    }\n    dist = sqrt(dist);\n    let label = C[b];\n    let diff = A[idx] - B[idx];\n    if (label > 0.5) {\n        output[idx] = 2.0 * diff / f32(params.batch_size);\n    } else {\n        let hinge = params.margin - dist;\n        if (hinge > 0.0 && dist > 1e-10) {\n            output[idx] = -2.0 * hinge * diff / (dist * f32(params.batch_size));\n        } else {\n            output[idx] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

}\n    let image_pos = idx % image_size;\n    let channel = (idx / image_size) % uf_params.channels;\n    let batch = idx / (uf_params.channels * image_size);\n    let ih = image_pos / uf_params.width;\n    let iw = image_pos % uf_params.width;\n    let window_size = uf_params.window_h * uf_params.window_w;\n    let col_channels = uf_params.channels * uf_params.kernel_h * uf_params.kernel_w;\n    var sum: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var kh: i32 = 0; }\n        for (var kw: i32 = 0; kw < uf_params.kernel_w; kw = kw + 1) {\n            let ow_numer = iw + uf_params.pad_w - kw;\n            if (ow_numer < 0 || ow_numer % uf_params.stride_w != 0) { continue; }\n            let ow = ow_numer / uf_params.stride_w;\n            if (ow >= uf_params.window_w) { continue; }\n            let col_row = (channel * uf_params.kernel_h + kh) * uf_params.kernel_w + kw;\n            let input_idx = batch * col_channels * window_size + col_row * window_size + oh * uf_params.window_w + ow;\n            sum = sum + uf_input[u32(input_idx)];\n        }\n    }\n    uf_output[gid.x] = sum;\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.Upsample3DSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Up3DParams {\n    batch_channels: u32,\n    in_d: u32, in_h: u32, in_w: u32,\n    scale_d: u32, scale_h: u32, scale_w: u32,\n    _pad: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: Up3DParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn nearest_upsample3d(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let out_d = params.in_d * params.scale_d;\n    let out_h = params.in_h * params.scale_h;\n    let out_w = params.in_w * params.scale_w;\n    let out_spatial = out_d * out_h * out_w;\n    let total = params.batch_channels * out_spatial;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let ow = idx % out_w;\n    let oh = (idx / out_w) % out_h;\n    let od = (idx / (out_w * out_h)) % out_d;\n    let nc = idx / out_spatial;\n    let id = od / params.scale_d;\n    let ih = oh / params.scale_h;\n    let iw = ow / params.scale_w;\n    output[idx] = input[(nc * params.in_d + id) * params.in_h * params.in_w + ih * params.in_w + iw];\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn nearest_upsample3d_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let in_spatial = params.in_d * params.in_h * params.in_w;\n    let total = params.batch_channels * in_spatial;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let iw = idx % params.in_w;\n    let ih = (idx / params.in_w) % params.in_h;\n    let id = (idx / (params.in_w * params.in_h)) % params.in_d;\n    let nc = idx / in_spatial;\n    let out_d = params.in_d * params.scale_d;\n    let out_h = params.in_h * params.scale_h;\n    let out_w = params.in_w * params.scale_w;\n    var sum_val: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var sd: u32 = 0u; sd < params.scale_d; sd = sd + 1u) {\n        for (var sh: u32 = 0u; sh < params.scale_h; sh = sh + 1u) {\n            for (var sw: u32 = 0u; sw < params.scale_w; sw = sw + 1u) {\n                let od = id * params.scale_d + sd;\n                let oh = ih * params.scale_h + sh;\n                let ow = iw * params.scale_w + sw;\n                sum_val = sum_val + input[(nc * out_d + od) * out_h * out_w + oh * out_w + ow];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    output[idx] = sum_val;\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.UpsampleSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    batch_channels: u32,\n    in_height: u32,\n    in_width: u32,\n    scale_h: u32,\n    scale_w: u32,\n    _pad1: u32,\n    _pad2: u32,\n    _pad3: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn nearest_upsample2d(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let out_h = params.in_height * params.scale_h;\n    let out_w = params.in_width * params.scale_w;\n    let total = params.batch_channels * out_h * out_w;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let ow = idx % out_w;\n    let oh = (idx / out_w) % out_h;\n    let bc = idx / (out_w * out_h);\n    let ih = oh / params.scale_h;\n    let iw = ow / params.scale_w;\n    output[idx] = input[bc * params.in_height * params.in_width + ih * params.in_width + iw];\n}\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn nearest_upsample2d_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    let total = params.batch_channels * params.in_height * params.in_width;\n    if (idx >= total) { return; }\n    let iw = idx % params.in_width;\n    let ih = (idx / params.in_width) % params.in_height;\n    let bc = idx / (params.in_width * params.in_height);\n    let out_w = params.in_width * params.scale_w;\n    let out_h = params.in_height * params.scale_h;\n    var acc: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var sh: u32 = 0u; sh < params.scale_h; sh = sh + 1u) {\n        for (var sw: u32 = 0u; sw < params.scale_w; sw = sw + 1u) {\n            let oh = ih * params.scale_h + sh;\n            let ow = iw * params.scale_w + sw;\n            acc = acc + input[bc * out_h * out_w + oh * out_w + ow];\n        }\n    }\n    output[idx] = acc;\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.VarAxisSource = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> input: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<f32>;\n\nstruct Params {\n    outer_size: u32,\n    reduce_size: u32,\n}\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> params: Params;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn var_axis(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let outer = gid.x;\n    if (outer >= params.outer_size) { return; }\n    let base = outer * params.reduce_size;\n    let N = f32(params.reduce_size);\n    var mean: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.reduce_size; i = i + 1u) {\n        mean = mean + input[base + i];\n    }\n    mean = mean / N;\n    var variance: f32 = 0.0;\n    for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < params.reduce_size; i = i + 1u) {\n        let d = input[base + i] - mean;\n        variance = variance + d * d;\n    }\n    output[outer] = variance / N;\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.WebGpu.WebGpuKernels.WhereBackward4Source = "\n@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> wb_a: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> wb_b: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> wb_c: array<f32>;\n@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> wb_d: array<f32>;\n\nstruct WBParams {\n    p0: u32,\n    pad0: u32,\n    pad1: u32,\n    pad2: u32,\n};\n@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> wb_params: WBParams;\n\n@compute @workgroup_size(256)\nfn where_backward(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {\n    let idx = gid.x;\n    if (idx < wb_params.p0) {\n        let cond = wb_b[idx];\n        wb_c[idx] = select(0.0, wb_a[idx], cond != 0.0);\n        wb_d[idx] = select(wb_a[idx], 0.0, cond != 0.0);\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
